Conclusion: PIVOT的贡献在于：提供了一个开源的真实多轨迹重建基准与处理流水线，系统地解耦轨迹、位姿和内参三个因素以诊断新视角合成模型的鲁棒性；方向性Chamfer距离为覆盖度提供了可量化的度量。其局限包括：当前v1仅含5个真实场景且数据来源单一（DJI Mini 4 Pro），评测聚焦于重建质量而不是下游任务（如感知、导航），且测量位姿的准确性受无人机传感器（IMU/GNSS）本身精度限制。

Abstract: Neural radiance fields (NeRFs), 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), and related novel-view synthesis methods are commonly evaluated under capture and reconstruction conditions cleaner than those encountered by robots, drones, and autonomous systems. Benchmarks often rely on reconstruction-friendly trajectories, optimized camera poses and intrinsics, and held-out views sampled from trajectories represented during training. These assumptions can obscure performance with measured poses, reusable camera calibration, and structurally different camera paths.
  We introduce PIVOT (Pose, Intrinsics and Viewpoint Oriented Testbed), a multi-trajectory dataset, processing pipeline, and evaluation framework for independently studying these factors. PIVOT captures each scene using diverse camera trajectories and retains, where available, both sensor-derived measured poses and COLMAP-optimized poses, together with calibrated and optimized camera intrinsics. It defines three benchmark families: (1) seen versus unseen trajectory novel-view generalization, (2) measured versus optimized pose sensitivity, and (3) calibrated versus optimized intrinsics sensitivity. We also introduce a directed pose-space Chamfer distance to quantify how well training poses cover an evaluation trajectory.
  PIVOT v1 contains five real-world scenes captured with a DJI Mini 4 Pro and provides an open processing and Nerfstudio-based evaluation toolchain. Benchmark results show a consistent quality gap between held-out views on represented trajectories and unseen trajectories, as well as substantial sensitivity to pose source and camera intrinsics.

</details>


### [27] [AdaptiveEmbed: Sample-Adaptive Multi-Vector Representation for Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25412)
*Xinze Liu,Lei Yang,Dayan Wu,Hengjie Zhu,Zihao Zhang,Hanqi Wu,Tianzhu Hu,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多模态检索问题设定——样本自适应多向量表示（SAMVR），以及其统一框架AdaptiveEmbed。该工作指出现有固定容量多向量表示（所有样本分配相同向量数）未能考虑样本个性化的检索需求，因此引入内容自适应嵌入集（CAES），通过多组对比学习（MGCL）学习结构化多向量表示，并通过边际效用分配（MUA）的效用策略优化（UPO）为每个样本决定最优向量容量。在图像、文本、视频、音频的多模态检索基准上，样本自适应容量分配优于固定容量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多向量表示（multi-vector representation）已成为多模态检索的主流范式，通过为每个样本生成多个互补嵌入来捕捉细粒度的跨模态信息。但现有方法采用固定表示容量，即所有样本都分配相同数量的向量，忽视了不同样本对表示能力的实际需求存在显著差异——某些样本只需少量向量即可实现有效检索，而另一些样本则需要更高容量。这种固定容量设定在表达能力和计算效率之间缺乏自适应权衡，是检索性能的整体瓶颈。让表示容量能够根据样本的具体检索难度和需求动态分配，是提升多模态检索性能的关键问题。

Method: AdaptiveEmbed框架包含三大核心组件：1) 内容自适应嵌入集（CAES）：每个样本由一个容量按其检索效用动态决定的嵌入集合表示，突破固定容量限制；2) 多组对比学习（MGCL）：将向量划分为多个互补组，通过对称的集合到集合相似度（SetSim）进行训练，使每组向量捕捉不同的细粒度语义层面，保证多向量表示的结构化与互补性；3) 效用策略优化（UPO）配合边际效用分配（MUA）：UPO通过强化学习/策略优化方式学习为每个样本决定需要多少表示向量，MUA则依据新增向量带来的边际检索效用增量来判定是否值得继续分配容量，从而在性能与开销间取得最优平衡。整体上，该方法将"表示容量"从全局超参数转化为可学习的样本级决策变量。

Result: 论文在涵盖图像、文本、视频和音频的多模态检索基准上进行了实验评估。核心结论是：样本自适应容量分配相比固定容量多向量表示取得了整体更优的检索性能，验证了SAMVR问题设定的有效性。通过让复杂样本获得更多向量、简单样本分配更少向量，AdaptiveEmbed在相同或更低的平均容量下实现了检索精度的提升，证明了边际效用分配策略能够识别哪些样本从额外表示向量中获益最多。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于：首次形式化定义样本自适应多向量表示（SAMVR）这一新问题设定，将表示容量从全局常数推广为样本级的可学习变量；提出AdaptiveEmbed统一框架，将多组对比学习、集合相似度计算和效用策略优化整合为端到端可学习的容量分配方案。该方法验证了"不同样本需要不同表示容量"这一直觉命题，为多模态检索的表示学习开辟了自适应容量分配的新方向。潜在的局限包括：边际效用的估计依赖近似的检索信号，可能在大规模动态数据分布下存在偏差；不同模态间容量分配策略的泛化性及计算开销的实际收益还有待更充分的验证。

Abstract: Multi-vector representations have emerged as an effective paradigm for multimodal retrieval, representing each sample with multiple complementary embeddings to capture fine-grained cross-modal information. However, existing approaches typically employ a fixed representation capacity, assigning the same number of vectors to all samples regardless of their individual retrieval demands. Such a fixed-capacity formulation overlooks the fact that different samples may require different amounts of representation capacity for effective retrieval. In this work, we introduce \emph{Sample-Adaptive Multi-Vector Representation} (SAMVR), a new problem setting for multimodal retrieval that studies how multi-vector representation capacity can be allocated at the sample level. Under SAMVR, each sample is represented by a \emph{content-adaptive embedding set} (CAES), whose capacity is determined according to the sample-specific retrieval utility of additional representation vectors. To instantiate SAMVR, we propose \emph{AdaptiveEmbed}, a unified framework for learning sample-adaptive multi-vector representations. AdaptiveEmbed learns structured multi-vector representations through \emph{Multi-Group Contrastive Learning} (MGCL) with the symmetric \emph{set-to-set similarity} (SetSim), and further employs \emph{Utility Policy Optimization} (UPO) to determine sample-specific representation capacity via \emph{Marginal Utility Allocation} (MUA). Experiments across multimodal retrieval benchmarks involving image, text, video, and audio show that sample-adaptive capacity allocation achieves overall better retrieval performance than fixed-capacity multi-vector representations, validating the effectiveness of SAMVR for multimodal retrieval. These results establish SAMVR as a viable formulation for adaptive capacity allocation in multi-vector multimodal retrieval.

</details>


### [28] [Bootstrapping a 4D LiDAR Annotation Tool from Video Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25418)
*Jihun Kim,Hyun-Kurl Jang,Hyemin Yang,Jinnyeong Yang,Hyeokjun Kweon,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 LiDAR-SAM2，一个利用 2D 视频基础模型 SAM2 自动为 4D LiDAR 点云序列生成训练标签的框架。通过多视图投影与时空聚合将 SAM2 的视频掩码转化为时序一致的 LiDAR 级标签，并设计专用的模态接口和两阶段学习目标，将 SAM2 的视频分割核适配到 LiDAR 时空结构，使单次点击即可获得跨序列一致的掩码轨迹。无需任何人工 LiDAR 标注即可逼近全人工标注的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D LiDAR 分割的发展受限于标注数据瓶颈：为稀疏点云序列分配时序一致的标签成本极高且难以扩展，而新任务或新域通常需要重新进行密集标注。这促使作者探索一个根本性问题——能否完全无需人工标注就自动生成高质量的 LiDAR 训练数据。该问题对 3D/4D 场景理解的大规模落地至关重要，因为当前监督学习的性能增长被昂贵的标注成本严重制约。

Method: LiDAR-SAM2 由数据和建模两部分构成。数据侧：将 SAM2 生成的视频掩码通过多视图投影（multi-view projection）和时空聚合（spatio-temporal aggregation）自动转化为时序连贯的 LiDAR 级标签，实现标签的自动生成。建模侧：设计了适配 LiDAR 模态的专用接口（modality interface），并采用两阶段学习目标（two-stage learning objective），将 SAM2 的视频分割核（video segmentation kernel）迁移到 LiDAR 的时空结构中。最终仅需对每个物体点击一次，即可在序列中获得一致的掩码轨迹。整个流程在无任何人工 LiDAR 标注下完成语义与全景标签的生成。

Result: 在 SemanticKITTI 数据集上，LiDAR-SAM2 生成的语义和全景标签仅需少量点即可逼近全人工标注的质量；基于这些标签训练的模型，其性能也接近使用完整 ground-truth 监督训练的模型。这证明 LiDAR-SAM2 作为可扩展标注工具能显著降低 3D/4D 场景理解的标注负担。实验主要围绕标签质量与下游模型训练的逼近度展开。

Conclusion: 核心贡献在于：(1) 提出将 2D 视频基础模型转化为 4D LiDAR 监督源的全新范式，打通了 2D 视觉与 3D 时序点云的标注鸿沟；(2) 设计了从视频掩码自动生成时序一致 LiDAR 标签的完整数据管线；(3) 通过模态接口与两阶段训练实现 SAM2 核的有效跨模态迁移。局限在于验证集中于 SemanticKITTI 单一数据集，其泛化性（其他传感器、新场景与新任务）尚待验证。

Abstract: Progress in 4D LiDAR segmentation is bottlenecked by data. Assigning temporally consistent labels across sparse point cloud sequences is costly and hard to scale, and every new task or domain tends to demand fresh dense annotation. This motivates a simple question of whether high-quality LiDAR training data can be produced automatically, without any human labeling. To this end, we introduce LiDAR-SAM2, a framework that turns a 2D video foundation model, SAM2, into a scalable source of supervision for the 4D LiDAR domain. On the data side, it automatically generates temporally coherent LiDAR-level labels from SAM2 video masks through multi-view projection and spatio-temporal aggregation. On the modeling side, a tailored modality interface and a two-stage learning objective adapt SAM2's video segmentation kernel to spatio-temporal LiDAR structure, so that a single click per object yields a consistent mask track across the sequence. Trained with no human LiDAR annotation, LiDAR-SAM2 produces semantic and panoptic labels on SemanticKITTI that approach the quality of full human annotation from only a few points, and models trained on these labels approach the performance of full ground-truth supervision. This positions LiDAR-SAM2 as a scalable labeling tool that substantially reduces the annotation burden for 3D and 4D scene understanding.

</details>


### [29] [Saliency-Depth Conditioning for Zero-Shot Segmentation of Communication-Tower Components in Cluttered UAV Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25435)
*Ali Lesani,Chul Min Yeum,Su-Min Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对无人机塔架巡检中通信塔组件细粒度分割任务，提出一种模型无关的显著性-深度前景条件化策略（SD Fg-conditioning），利用外观显著性图与单目相对深度构建粗糙塔架先验以抑制杂乱背景干扰。该方法与Grounded-SAM和SAM 3集成，得到SD-Grounded-SAM和SD-SAM 3。SD-Grounded-SAM额外采用几何与深度感知的边界框细化，而SD-SAM 3依赖SAM 3内部机制。在包含340张无人机塔架图像的TOW-300数据集上，SD-SAM 3取得最强实例分割性能，SD-Grounded-SAM产生更少误检。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信塔组件（如绝缘子、天线等）的细粒度分割对无人机自动化巡检至关重要。但实例级标注稀缺，导致训练专用模型成本高昂。零样本分割模型（如Grounded-SAM、SAM 3）提供替代方案，但在杂乱场景中，与塔架视觉特征相似的背景结构会干扰组件定位，导致漏检（missed instances）与误检（false positives）。因此核心动机是：如何在不依赖任务特定标注的情况下，利用可泛化的提示条件（foreground conditioning）增强零样本分割模型在杂乱场景中的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出一个模型无关（model-agnostic）的显著性-深度前景条件化策略，融合两类互补线索：1) 基于外观的显著性图（appearance-based saliency）用于突出塔架前景区域；2) 单目相对深度（monocular relative depth）用于区分塔架与背景结构的空间层次。二者结合得到粗糙塔架先验（coarse tower prior），通过抑制无关背景内容约束后续分割模型。该模块作为前置条件注入Grounded-SAM得到SD-Grounded-SAM，在掩码生成前额外应用几何与深度感知的框细化（geometric and depth-aware box refinement）以提高定位精度；注入SAM 3得到SD-SAM 3，利用SAM 3内部设置。先验、深度、框细化三个组件各自贡献互补增益。

Result: 实验基于TOW-300数据集，包含340张通信塔无人机图像。评估指标涉及实例分割性能与误检率。结果表明：所提策略一致改善两个基线。SD-SAM 3取得最强的实例分割性能；SD-Grounded-SAM产生更少的误检（false positives）。消融实验确认显著性、深度与框细化三者互补，共同提升在杂乱场景中的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出一种无需任务特定标注、可与主流零样本SAM系列模型即插即用的前景条件化策略，验证了外观显著性与单目深度在构造目标先验中的互补价值，为无人机塔架巡检等细粒度实例感知场景提供了高效增强路径。局限：基于相对深度而非度量深度，框细化依赖几何约束假设；目前仅在塔架类目标上验证，泛化到其他杂乱结构场景的普适性有待进一步探索。

Abstract: Fine-grained segmentation of communication-tower components in UAV imagery is essential for automated inspection, yet task-specific models are hard to develop due to limited instance-level annotations. Zero-shot segmentation models offer a promising alternative, but in cluttered scenes, visually similar background structures interfere with component localization, causing missed instances and false positives. We propose a model-agnostic saliency-depth foreground-conditioning strategy combining appearance-based saliency with monocular relative depth to construct a coarse tower prior and suppress irrelevant content. We integrate this module with Grounded-SAM and SAM 3, yielding SD-Grounded-SAM and SD-SAM 3. SD-Grounded-SAM further applies geometric and depth-aware box refinement before mask generation, while SD-SAM 3 relies on SAM 3's internal setup. On TOW-300, a dataset of 340 communication-tower UAV images, our strategy improves both baselines: SD-SAM 3 achieves the strongest instance-segmentation performance, while SD-Grounded-SAM produces fewer false positives. Ablations confirm complementary gains from saliency, depth, and box refinement, improving robustness in cluttered scenes.

</details>


### [30] [VGA-BenchV2: An Expanded Unified Benchmark and Multi-Model Framework for Evaluating Video Aesthetics and Generation Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25452)
*Longteng Jiang,DanDan Zheng,Qianqian Qiao,Heng Huang,Huaye Wang,Yihang Bo,Bao Peng,Jingdong Chen,Jun Zhou,Xin Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VGA-BenchV2 是一个扩展的人类对齐基准与优化框架，用于联合评估和提升视频生成质量与美学价值。它在 VGA-Bench 基础上保留了"Aesthetic（美学）"和"Generation（生成质量）"两个主维度及52个子维度的细粒度分类体系，新增1,016个提示和超过60,000个由12个主流视频生成模型生成的视频，并额外加入36,000条任务级人工标注。论文提出一个混合评估器架构（VAQA-Net 连续美学评分 + 两个基于 Qwen 的大视觉语言模型 VTag-Net 和 VGQA-Net 分别用于美学标签和生成质量评估），并进一步构建了"评估到优化"（evaluation-to-optimization）流水线，将学到的美学评估器作为强化学习生成器微调的奖励模型，形成从基准构建、人工监督到自动化评估和模型优化的闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型的快速发展带来了对质量与美学双重评估的迫切需求。已有的 VGA-Bench 提供了细粒度的评估分类体系，但其人工标注规模有限，难以支撑鲁棒、与人类判断高度对齐的评估器训练；同时，评估基准与生成模型的优化往往是割裂的——评估结果未能反向指导生成模型的改进。该论文旨在通过大幅扩充人工标注数据、构建混合评估器架构，并打通"评估→优化"的闭环，使视频生成模型不仅能提升真实性，还能在美学质量与人类偏好对齐上获得可量化、可优化的改进。

Method: VGA-BenchV2 包含三大技术贡献：(1) 数据与标注体系：保留原有两维度（Aesthetic、Generation）和52个子维度的细粒度分类学，重新策划1,016个多样化提示，收集12个主流视频生成模型产生的60,000+视频，并新增36,000条任务级人工标注（16,200条美学质量、13,200条美学标签、6,600条生成质量，分别是 VGA-Bench 的13.46x、11.15x和1.55x）。(2) 混合评估器架构：包含用于连续美学评分的 VAQA-Net，以及两个基于 Qwen 大视觉语言模型的评估器——VTag-Net（负责美学标签标注）和 VGQA-Net（负责生成质量评估），实现多维度、分任务的人类对齐评估。(3) 评估到优化流水线：将学到的美学评估器作为强化学习（RL）奖励模型，用于视频生成器的微调，构成从基准构建、人工监督到自动化评估与模型优化的完整闭环。

Result: 论文在12个主流视频生成模型生成的60,000+视频上开展实验，利用扩充后的人工标注语料训练混合评估器，并通过多种指标验证评估结果与人类判断在多样生成模型上的强一致性。具体实验设置与数值指标在摘要中未详细给出，但强调混合评估器在跨模型场景下表现出对人类判断的强对齐性。此外，评估到优化流水线的实验表明，将美学评估器作为RL奖励模型进行生成器微调，能够使视频生成器在现实主义、美学质量和人类偏好对齐方面同时获得改进，验证了闭环优化框架的有效性。

Conclusion: VGA-BenchV2 的主要贡献在于：大幅扩充了视频生成质量与美学评估的人工标注语料，提出融合判别式评分网络（VAQA-Net）与两个大视觉语言模型评估器的混合架构，实现更精确、更细粒度、与人类判断高度对齐的视频评估；并创新性地引入"评估到优化"闭环，将评估器作为RL奖励模型驱动生成器微调。可能存在的局限包括：摘要未给出具体性能数值与对比基线，需进一步查阅正文验证评估器绝对精度、RL优化的收敛性与鲁棒性，以及评估器在未见过的更新款生成模型上的泛化能力。基准与资源已公开于 HuggingFace。

Abstract: We introduce VGA-BenchV2, an extended human-aligned benchmark and optimization framework for jointly evaluating and improving video generation quality and aesthetic value. Built upon VGA-Bench, VGA-BenchV2 preserves the original fine-grained taxonomy with two primary dimensions-Aesthetic and Generation-and 52 sub-dimensions. Guided by this taxonomy, we curate 1,016 diverse prompts and collect over 60,000 videos generated by 12 mainstream video generation models. More importantly, VGA-BenchV2 substantially expands human-labeled supervision by adding 36,000 task-level annotations, including 16,200 for aesthetic quality, 13,200 for aesthetic tagging, and 6,600 for generation quality, corresponding to 13.46x, 11.15x, and 1.55x scale-ups over VGA-Bench, respectively. Leveraging this enlarged annotation corpus, we develop a hybrid evaluator architecture consisting of VAQA-Net for continuous aesthetic scoring and two Qwen-based Large Vision-Language Model evaluators, VTag-Net and VGQA-Net, for aesthetic tagging and generation quality assessment. Extensive experiments demonstrate strong alignment with human judgments across diverse generation models. Beyond evaluation, VGA-BenchV2 further introduces an evaluation-to-optimization pipeline, where the learned aesthetic evaluator serves as a reward model for reinforcement learning-based generator fine-tuning. This closes the loop from benchmark construction and human supervision to automated evaluation and model optimization, enabling video generators to improve not only in realism but also in aesthetic quality and human preference alignment. Resources are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/BestiVictoryLab/VGA-Bench.

</details>


### [31] [Automatic weld seam segmentation for industrial quality control: a comparison of RGB and polarimetric imaging with CNN and transformer architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25465)
*Simone Garbin,Leonardo Venturoso,Marco Todescato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了从RGB和偏振图像对焊接装配体进行自动焊缝分割的可行性，在受控实验室与真实非受控条件下系统比较了CNN和Transformer架构。核心发现是：采集设置是检测系统的一阶因素（受控RGB的mAP50最高0.87，非受控降至0.22-0.48）；采用对齐保持几何增强的偏振成像无需采集控制即可达到与最优受控RGB相当的分割精度（mAP50最高0.93）；架构上Transformer（尤其RF-DETR）对测试时视点偏移具有显著鲁棒性，而CNN在此情形下性能崩溃。以三种随机种子统一、阈值无关的协议评价，排除了种子噪声与训练分辨率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 焊接装配体的目视检测是许多工业生产流程中最少自动化的环节之一，高度依赖人工操作者经验，存在操作员间差异。本研究以特种机械舱室制造为应用场景，旨在评估自动焊缝分割的可行性，其核心动机在于量化(i)采集条件（受控 vs 非受控）对模型性能的影响，以及(ii)与采集设置解耦的、真正来自模型架构（CNN vs Transformer）的性能差异，从而为实际产线部署提供可推广的指导。

Method: 该工作提出了一套统一的、阈值无关（threshold-independent）的评测协议，以消除评估中的主观阈值和标准差异。对此协议下，CNN架构与Transformer架构在相同条件下被公平基准测试，每个CNN使用三个随机种子训练，以将真实效应与随机初始化造成的种子噪声分离。方法上的两个关键技术是：(1) 偏振成像（polarimetric imaging）结合对齐保持（alignment-preserving）的几何增强，用于在非受控条件下可靠分割此前未见过的焊缝；(2) 分辨率匹配控制（resolution-matched control）实验，用以区分模型架构本身与训练分辨率对性能的影响。最终得到关于架构与视点鲁棒性的可解释结论。

Result: 实验对比了受控实验室采集与非受控真实采集的RGB与偏振图像。受控RGB条件下，CNN的平均掩膜mAP50最高达0.87；但非受控采集下骤降至0.22-0.48，证实采集设置是系统的一阶因素。采用对齐保持几何增强的偏振成像在非受控RGB下对未见焊缝定位的平均掩膜mAP50最高达0.93，与最优受控RGB结果持平（而非超越），但无需采集控制。架构层面的关键发现是视点鲁棒性：分布内（in-distribution）条件下Transformer与CNN性能相当，但在测试时视点偏移（viewpoint shift）下，Transformer模型保持高精度，而所有CNN均崩溃。该差距在三种随机种子与分辨率匹配对照下均成立，表明差异源于架构而非训练分辨率。在CNN家族内部，一旦计入种子方差，更大容量并未带来可靠的分布内增益——小规模CNN足以应对固定视点，可变视点则需要Transformer。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：(1) 建立了统一、阈值无关的焊缝分割评测协议，为同类工业检测研究提供可复现的参照标准；(2) 量化证明了采集设置（受控 vs 非受控）是影响检测精度的首要因素，削弱了单纯的算法改进价值；(3) 展示了偏振成像结合对齐保持几何增强在无需严格采集控制下逼近受控RGB性能的可行性，对产线布线具有实用意义；(4) 通过随机种子与分辨率匹配对照，严谨地将视点鲁棒性归因于架构（Transformer/RF-DETR）而非训练配置。局限性方面：偏振成像只是与受控RGB性能齐平而非超越，且研究限于单一工业场景（特种机械舱室），其结论的泛化性有待更多场景验证。

Abstract: Visual inspection of welded assemblies remains one of the least automated stages in many industrial production processes, still depending largely on the experience of human operators and thus subject to inter-operator variability; the manufacturing of special-purpose machinery cabins, the setting of this study, is one representative case. This work evaluates the feasibility of automatic weld seam segmentation from RGB and polarimetric imagery, comparing controlled laboratory acquisitions with images captured under real, uncontrolled conditions. Convolutional neural network (CNN) architectures and transformer-based architectures are benchmarked under a unified, threshold-independent protocol, training each CNN with three random seeds to separate genuine effects from seed noise. In controlled RGB conditions, CNN models reach a mean mask mAP50 of up to 0.87, but drop to 0.22-0.48 under uncontrolled acquisition, showing that the acquisition setup is a first-order component of the inspection system. Polarimetric imaging with alignment-preserving geometric augmentation localizes previously unseen welds with a mean mask mAP50 up to 0.93: on par with, rather than ahead of, the best controlled-RGB result, but reaching that accuracy on uncontrolled RGB without requiring acquisition control. The clearest architectural finding concerns viewpoint robustness. In-distribution, transformers and CNNs are broadly comparable; but under a test-time viewpoint shift, the transformer models, and RF-DETR in particular, retain high accuracy while every CNN collapses. The gap holds across three seeds and a resolution-matched control, pointing to architecture rather than training resolution. Within the CNN family, capacity brings no reliable in-distribution gain once seed variance is accounted for: small CNNs suffice for fixed viewpoints, transformers for variable ones.

</details>


### [32] [PAGS: Autofocusing Photoacoustic Tomography via Speed-of-Sound-Adaptive Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25472)
*Jiarui Ge,Jintao Ma,Bangxu Fan,Jinyan Zhang,Xiaokang Yang,Shuai Na,Xiaoyun Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PAGS（Photoacoustic Autofocusing via Gaussian Splatting）是一个用于光声计算机断层成像（PACT）的盲自动对焦可微分框架。它利用稀疏高斯光声源表示初始压力场，并用球谐探针参数化的各向异性路径平均声速（ASoS）场替代显式介质恢复，通过解析高斯声学投影实现信号域闭环优化，在无需校准声速先验的情况下同步优化声源和声速场参数，提升异构声学介质下的重建清晰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PACT将光学吸收对比与声学检测结合，实现高分辨率深层组织成像。核心挑战在于未知声速（SoS）非均匀性会改变声学飞行时间，当重建假设均匀声速时会产生散焦伪影。现有方法要么依赖标定的声学先验，要么优化稠密的物理介质模型，在3D场景中计算昂贵且难以扩展。因此需要一种无需先验、可扩展的盲自动对焦策略。

Method: PAGS的核心创新有三：1）用稀疏高斯光声（PA）源表示初始压力场，替代体素网格表示；2）用球谐探针参数化的紧凑各向异性路径平均声速（ASoS）场替代显式介质恢复，该隐式传播场直接控制源到换能器的到达时间对齐；3）提出解析高斯声学投影函数，将高斯源表示高效映射到换能器信号。由此构成闭环信号域优化：从实测数据同时更新高斯PA源参数与ASoS场，全程无需标定声速先验。

Result: 实验在仿真和物理仿体数据上进行，验证了异构声学介质下重建锐度的改善，对稀疏视角采样的鲁棒性，以及解析高斯投影带来的计算效率优势。结果表明PAGS相比传统均匀声速重建在去散焦伪影方面表现更优。

Conclusion: PAGS的主要贡献是提出一种无需先验、可微分的盲自动对焦PACT框架，将3D高斯泼溅思想引入光声成像，用紧凑ASoS场隐式建模声速异质性。其意义在于兼顾重建质量、稀疏采样鲁棒性和计算效率，可扩展至3D。局限可能包括依赖高斯源表示对复杂压力场的表达能力，以及ASoS球谐参数化对剧烈声速突变介质建模的精度限制。

Abstract: Photoacoustic computed tomography (PACT) combines optical absorption contrast with acoustic detection for high-resolution deep-tissue imaging. A persistent challenge is that unknown speed-of-sound (SoS) heterogeneity changes acoustic time-of-flight, causing defocusing artifacts when reconstruction assumes a uniform SoS. Existing SoS-adaptive methods either rely on calibrated acoustic priors or optimize dense physical medium models, which becomes expensive and difficult to scale in 3D. We propose PAGS, a differentiable framework for blind autofocusing PACT via speed-of-sound-adaptive Gaussian splatting. PAGS represents the initial pressure field with sparse Gaussian photoacoustic (PA) sources and replaces explicit medium recovery with a compact anisotropic path-averaged SoS (ASoS) field parameterized by spherical harmonic probes. This latent propagation field directly controls source-to-transducer arrival-time alignment, while an analytic Gaussian acoustic projection maps the source representation to transducer signals efficiently. The resulting closed-loop signal-domain optimization jointly updates the Gaussian PA source parameters and the ASoS field from measured data, without calibrated SoS priors. Experiments on simulated and physical phantom data demonstrate improved reconstruction sharpness under heterogeneous acoustic media, robustness to sparse-view sampling, and computational benefits from the analytic Gaussian projection.

</details>


### [33] [4DStreamCtrl: Interactive Video Generation with Online 4D Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25479)
*Shiqian Li,Chenguo Lin,Zhiguang Liu,Yu Tang,Jiarong Ou,Rui Chen,Yixin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 4DStreamCtrl，一种统一的 3D 点轨迹（point-track）视频生成表示方法，将相机运动、物体轨迹和深度统一到一个单一表示中，使得单个预训练视频扩散模型能够在一遍前向传播中同时执行相机与物体联合控制、深度编辑和运动迁移。通过引入时间可分离的轻量级 Geometric Motion Head，该方法可蒸馏为因果流式学生模型，在 4 步去噪下以与视频长度无关的内存生成任意长度的视频流，在单张高端 GPU 上以 20 FPS 生成 480p 视频，首次实现了交互式 4D 可控的流式视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式视频模型的交互控制能力存在严重割裂：相机参数类方法只能控制视点但无法移动物体，2D 轨迹方法只在图像平面内作用并忽略深度与遮挡关系，而最近的 3D 方法虽然引入几何信息却只能离线生成固定长度视频。没有任何现有方法能够同时实现：(1) 对相机和物体的 3D 一致性控制，(2) 实时流式生成。这一能力缺口阻碍了视频生成模型作为交互式工具（如可控模拟器、具身智能体的实时视觉想象）的实际应用，因此需要一种统一且高效的几何控制与生成框架。

Method: 核心技术思路是将相机运动、物体轨迹和深度统合到一个 3D 点轨迹（3D point-track）表示中，使单个模型即可联合完成相机与物体控制、深度编辑与运动迁移。具体包括三大组成部分：(1) 数据层面，通过挖掘互联网视频（in-the-wild video）提取 3D 运动监督信号，构建了大规模数据集 OpenVidHD-Motion3D；(2) 架构层面，设计了一个轻量级 Geometric Motion Head 对 3D 运动条件进行编码，可即插即用地接入预训练的视频扩散模型；(3) 推理层面，利用该编码器的时间可分离性（temporally separable），将模型蒸馏为因果流式学生模型（causal streaming student），支持以固定内存（与长度无关）在 4 步去噪中生成任意长度视频，实现实时流式生成。

Result: 实验表明 4DStreamCtrl 在运动控制精度上全面超越仅相机控制方法、纯 2D 轨迹方法以及离线 3D 方法，同时覆盖了上述方法仅能单独处理的多种控制模态（相机、物体、深度、运动迁移）。实际性能方面，模型在单张高端 GPU 上以 20 FPS 生成 480p 视频，并能保持数百帧的时序一致性（temporal coherence），实现了此前不可行的交互式 4D 可控流式生成。研究还验证了因果流式蒸馏在长视频生成中保持时间一致性与效率的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献在于首次将相机运动、物体轨迹与深度统一为单一 3D 点轨迹表示，在一遍前向中支持多模态几何控制，并通过时间可分离编码与因果流式蒸馏实现了与长度无关的实时长视频生成。该工作将显式 3D 几何约束与高效因果推理相结合，为交互式世界模型（closed-loop spatiotemporal control）指明了方向，可应用于可控模拟器与具身智能体的实时视觉想象。潜在局限包括：依赖数据挖掘得到的 3D 监督信号质量，在极端遮挡和复杂场景下 3D 轨迹估计的准确性可能受限；流式蒸馏可能导致长程外观漂移或细节保真度下降；性能验证主要在单一高端 GPU 上，边缘实时部署仍有挑战。论文未给出与事件相机/脉冲神经网络等方向的直接联系，但对实时、流式、几何可控生成的需求与低延迟感知生成闭环高度相关。

Abstract: Generative video models now synthesize footage nearly indistinguishable from reality. Their promise as interactive tools hinges on fine-grained control of how objects and the camera move over time, yet each existing approach captures only part of this: camera-parameter methods steer the viewpoint but cannot move objects, 2D-trajectory methods act in the image plane and ignore depth and occlusion, and recent 3D methods add geometry but run only offline at a fixed length. In particular, none combines 3D-consistent control of both camera and objects with real-time, streaming generation. Here we show that camera motion, object trajectories, and depth can be unified into a single 3D point-track representation, from which one model performs joint camera and object control, depth editing, and motion transfer in a single forward pass. To learn this interface at scale, we mine in-the-wild video for 3D motion supervision, yielding OpenVidHD-Motion3D, and encode it with a lightweight Geometric Motion Head that plugs into a pretrained video diffusion model. Because this encoder is temporally separable, we distill the model into a causal streaming student that generates arbitrarily long video in four denoising steps at memory independent of length. This unified design surpasses prior camera-only, 2D, and offline-3D methods in motion-control precision while covering modalities they address only in isolation. 4DStreamCtrl runs at 20 FPS on a single high-end GPU for 480p video and stays temporally coherent over hundreds of frames, enabling, to our knowledge, interactive 4D-controllable streaming generation for the first time. More broadly, grounding generation in explicit 3D geometry with efficient causal inference points toward interactive world models with closed-loop spatiotemporal control, from controllable simulators to real-time visual imagination for embodied agents.

</details>


### [34] [DeCO: Discriminative Evidence Composition for Fine-Grained Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25480)
*Chuixuan Fan,Guang Li,Shijie Wang,Dongzhan Zhou,Baoli Sun,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama,Zhihui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出数据集蒸馏新方法 DeCO（Discriminative Evidence Composition），将细粒度视觉分类（FGVC）的数据集蒸馏问题形式化为"预算受限的判别性证据保留"问题。利用预训练 TransFG 教师的注意力卷展（attention rollout）识别信息丰富的图像块，结合空间多样化减少冗余覆盖，并将同类区域组织成类别证据库，最终将多个同类区域打包成紧凑的网格组合图像。教师仅用于数据集构建，下游学生模型使用标准硬标签监督训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集蒸馏方法主要保留全局图像统计信息（如全局特征分布、类原型等），可能忽略细粒度视觉分类（FGVC）中至关重要的局部判别证据，如物体部件、细微纹理和区域特定结构。FGVC 任务的目标类别差异往往仅体现在微小的局部区域（如鸟类的喙、飞机机翼细节），全局统计可能使合成图像丧失这些关键判别信息。因此需要一种针对 FGVC 设计的数据集蒸馏方法，在有限 IPC（每类图像数量）预算下显式保留局部判别证据。

Method: DeCO 方法包含三步流水线：（1）使用预训练 TransFG 教师的注意力卷展（attention rollout）来识别信息性高的图像块，即根据注意力传播分数确定最具判别力的局部区域；（2）对这些区域施加空间多样化（spatial diversification），减少冗余覆盖，确保选取的判别区域互补而非重叠；（3）将筛选出的区域按类别整理成类别证据库（class-wise evidence banks），再打包为紧凑的网格组合图像（grid-composed images）。值得注意的是，TransFG 教师仅用于离线数据集构建，训练阶段下游学生模型采用标准硬标签监督，不依赖教师 logits 蒸馏，从而避免了推理时对教师模型的依赖。

Result: 实验在三个标准 FGVC 基准上进行：CUB-200-2011（鸟类细粒度分类）、FGVC-Aircraft（飞机型号分类）和 Stanford Cars（汽车型号分类）。在不同 IPC（每类图像数量）预算下，DeCO 一致优于代表性的 coreset 方法和数据集蒸馏基线方法。结果表明，通过显式构建含判别局部证据的合成图像，DeCO 在更紧凑的合成集下取得了更好的细粒度分类精度，验证了"局部判别证据保留"相比"全局统计保留"对 FGVC 数据集蒸馏更有效。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：（1）将 FGVC 数据集蒸馏重新形式化为预算受限的判别性证据保留问题；（2）提出 DeCO 方法，结合注意力卷展、空间多样化和网格组合图像生成，实现对局部判别证据的显式保留；（3）通过广泛的 FGVC 基准实验证明其优于现有蒸馏与 coreset 基线。局限性：依赖预训练 TransFG 教师来获取注意力信号，引入额外的预训练成本；当前仅在静态图像分类上验证，尚未扩展到事件流视觉或时空序列感知场景；注意力卷展选取的块质量受教师模型能力制约。

Abstract: Dataset distillation compresses a large training set into a compact synthetic set while preserving its downstream utility. However, existing methods primarily preserve global image statistics and may overlook the localized evidence essential for fine-grained visual classification (FGVC), such as object parts, subtle textures, and region-specific structures. We formulate fine-grained dataset distillation as budgeted discriminative-evidence preservation and propose Discriminative Evidence Composition (DeCO). DeCO uses attention rollout from a pretrained TransFG teacher to identify informative patches, applies spatial diversification to reduce redundant coverage, and organizes the resulting regions into class-wise evidence banks. Multiple same-class regions are then packed into compact grid-composed images. The teacher is used only for dataset construction, whereas downstream students are trained with standard hard-label supervision without teacher logits. Experiments on CUB-200-2011, FGVC-Aircraft, and Stanford Cars show that DeCO consistently outperforms representative coreset and dataset-distillation baselines under different IPC budgets.

</details>


### [35] [Gaussian Splatting Underwater: A Controlled Cross-Regime Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25483)
*Olaya Álvarez-Tuñón,Stella Graßhof*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对高斯泼溅（3DGS）方法在真实水下环境中的性能进行了系统性基准评估。作者建立了统一的评估协议（共享位姿、初始化、预算和评估器），在多个公开水下数据集及工业勘测数据上比较了五个公开代码系统（包括medium-blind 3DGS和medium-aware方案）。核心发现是：水下3D重建的性能主要取决于数据采集setup（水体清晰度和光照几何），而非网络架构。水体浑浊度在渲染前就决定了重建可行性（SfM在清水注册99.5%的帧，12 NTU下为0%）；当人工光源跟随相机移动时，medium-blind方法反而优于medium-aware方法；在工业勘测数据上，基准的photometric最佳方法在几何上垫底。论文开放了全部场景构建、配置和评估代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境对3D重建极具挑战：悬浮粒子散射和漫射光、浊度变化、波长相关的光吸收、以及不均匀照明。现有基于高斯泼溅的方法大多针对良好成像质量的条件开发，且主要测试于相对浅水，缺乏在真实水下退化条件下（不同浊度、光照损失、颜色衰减）的系统性、公平比较。此前的领域内排行榜分数无法反映实际部署中的真实表现，因此需要一个统一协议下的横向基准，以揭示各方法的真实优势、劣势与局限，并为水下感知应用提供可靠的方法选型依据。

Method: 论文不提出新模型，而是构建一个统一、严格可控的基准评估框架。核心方法要素：(1) 统一协议：五个公开系统在相同位姿、初始化、训练预算和评估器下运行，消除实现差异带来的公平性问题；(2) 多样化数据覆盖：使用多个公开水下数据集模拟不同程度的浊度、光照损失和颜色衰减，并包含一个工业勘测数据集；(3) 系统性因素分解：将水体清晰度（NTU浊度）、光照几何（光源是否随相机移动）作为自变量，观察各方法性能的敏感度；(4) 多维度评估：区分photometric指标与geometry指标，避免单一分数掩盖几何性能差异；(5) 端到端管线分析：将SfM（运动恢复结构）帧注册率作为前序条件，解释为何部分方法在浑浊水中完全失效（上游制约），并指出restoration预处理与vanilla 3DGS组合在几何上的优势。

Result: 实验在多个公开水下数据集和工业勘测上进行，评估五个公开代码系统。关键结果：(1) 框架注册率随浊度急剧下降——清水下SfM注册99.5%的帧，而在12 NTU浊度下降至0.0%，说明水体清晰度是渲染性能的上游硬约束；(2) 光照几何决定medium model是否有效：当人工光与相机同向移动时，medium-blind（不考虑介质的）泼溅方法优于两个medium-aware（考虑介质模型的）系统；(3) 在工业勘测上，该领域基准中photometric排名第一的方法在几何指标上垫底，而被几何评估击败它的是"restoration预处理+vanilla 3DGS"组合；(4) 这些关键差异在传统报告分数中完全不可见，凸显了评估指标选择对结论的影响。

Conclusion: 本文的重要贡献在于揭示了水下高斯泼溅3D重建中的关键瓶颈并非网络架构，而是数据采集与预处理setup：水体浊度通过SfM帧注册率在上游决定方法的有效期，照明几何（光源是否随相机移动）决定是否值得引入介质模型。研究还指出当前benchmark的photometric评分会误导方法选型（几何性能被掩盖）。局限性包括：评估范围限于五个公开系统，未覆盖全部最新工作；对深层浑浊水下的极端条件下方法完全失效，未给出可用的恢复方案；结论的普适性依赖所选数据集与勘测场景的代表性。该工作为水下3D重建提供了一个可复现、公平的评估基线和明确的方法选用指南。

Abstract: The underwater environment is challenging for 3D reconstruction, because particles suspended in the water scatter and diffuse light, turbidity varies, absorption depends on wavelength, and illumination is rarely uniform. Methods based on Gaussian splatting have generally been developed for conditions that allow good image quality, and have primarily been tested on relatively shallow water. This paper examines how well Gaussian splatting performs across publicly available underwater datasets representing different degrees of turbidity, loss of illumination, and colour attenuation, together with an industrial survey. Five systems with public code are run under one protocol, with shared poses, initialisation, budget, and evaluator, to establish their relative advantages, disadvantages, and limitations. What these methods can do turns out to depend more on the setup than on the architecture. Water clarity binds upstream of rendering, since structure-from-motion registers 99.5 \% of frames in clear water and 0.0 \% at 12 NTU. Illumination geometry decides whether a medium model helps at all: under an artificial light that moves with the camera, medium-blind splatting beats both medium-aware systems. On the survey the benchmark's photometric leader comes last, beaten on geometry by a restoration pre-pass in front of vanilla 3DGS---and none of it is visible in the scores the field reports. Scene builds, per-run configurations, and evaluation code are released at https://github.com/olayasturias/uw3dgs

</details>


### [36] [Semi-Supervised Adaptation of Vision-Language Models for Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25485)
*Mohamed L. Mekhalfi,Mohamad M. Al Rahhal,Yakoub Bazi,Salah E. Khenfer,Mingdeng Shi,Hua Zou,Mansour Zuair*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Evolutionary CLIP (SE-CLIP)，一种面向遥感场景分类的半监督框架，通过"热启动+递归标签挖掘"双阶段流程，将视觉-语言模型(如CLIP)从自然图像领域自适应到遥感领域。其核心创新在于类平衡选择策略，确保挖掘样本的类别均衡性，避免模型被易学类别主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等视觉-语言模型在自然图像上表现优异，但在卫星/遥感图像上因数据分布差异（如视角、尺度、光谱特性）表现受限。虽然已有参数高效微调(parameter-efficient adaptation)技术，但遥感领域标注样本稀缺严重制约了这些方法的有效性。因此，如何在极少标注样本条件下将VLM有效适应到遥感场景，是一个关键挑战。

Method: SE-CLIP采用双阶段半监督管道：(1) 热启动阶段，在少量标注种子样本上对CLIP进行预热训练；(2) 递归发现阶段，迭代从未标注池中筛选高置信度样本扩充支持集。为了维护支持集的完整性，设计了类平衡选择策略，防止模型被易学习类别主导，实现递归标签挖掘。

Result: 在UCM和NWPU两个遥感场景分类基准上验证，SE-CLIP显著优于现有半监督方法。这证明了递归标签挖掘与类平衡策略在遥感VLM适配中的有效性。

Conclusion: SE-CLIP为在最小人工干预下将VLM适配到遥感领域提供了可行的解决方案。其限制可能包括：依赖置信度阈值设定质量、未涉及事件相机等非传统视觉模态，以及验证基准仅覆盖遥感场景分类任务，泛化性有待进一步探索。

Abstract: Vision-language models like CLIP have shown sig- nificant potential in handling natural images, yet their perfor- mance is often limited by the distinct characteristics of satellite imagery. While parameter-efficient adaptation techniques exist, their efficacy is frequently limited by the scarcity of annotated samples. In this letter, we propose Self-Evolutionary CLIP (SE- CLIP), a semi-supervised framework designed for recursive label mining in scene classification. The approach follows a dual-phase pipeline, where an initial warm-up on a few annotated seeds is followed by a recursive discovery phase that iteratively identifies high-confidence samples from unlabeled pools. To maintain the integrity of the evolving support set, we employ a class-balanced selection strategy that prevents the model from being dominated by easily learned categories. Results on the UCM and NWPU benchmarks indicate that SE-CLIP significantly outperforms existing semi-supervised approaches. The framework provides a viable solution for adapting VLMs to the remote sensing domain with minimal human intervention.

</details>


### [37] [SMART: MLLM-guided Temporal Alignment for Unifying Sign Language Recognition and Spotting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25493)
*Eunjee Choi,JungHoon Sung,Seongwhan Cho,Chu Xin,Younggeun Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SMART框架，一个基于多模态大语言模型(MLLM)引导的时序对齐框架，用于联合实现连续手语识别(CSLR)与手语词定位(spotting)。利用MLLM生成的语义运动描述和边界感知定位网络，在弱监督序列级标注下增强细粒度时序表示学习，并在四个手语基准上验证了识别与定位任务的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前连续手语识别方法依赖句子级标注，仅提供有限的时序和语义引导，难以支持细粒度表示学习。同时，常规视频-文本对齐需要大batch size，这对内存密集的手语视频训练非常低效。此外，传统CSLR方法缺少密集时序定位能力，未能充分利用识别信息的互补性。这些局限使得弱监督下的手语理解难以同时获得高质量的特征表示与精确的时序定位。

Method: SMART框架包含三个核心创新：(1) 利用MLLM生成的手语运动描述作为辅助语义线索，在小型batch训练条件下实现稳定的视频-文本对齐，突破了传统对比学习中大batch size的依赖；(2) 提出多尺度时序适配器(Multi-Scale Temporal Adapter)，在transformer编码过程中建模多粒度时序交互，增强时序表示学习；(3) 提出CSFormer——一种CSLR引导的定位模块，将识别过程推导出的词汇证据注入边界感知的定位网络，实现统一框架。该框架使CSLR特征辅助定位，同时定位监督信息反过来补充弱CTC识别的不足，形成双向增益。

Result: 在四个手语基准数据集上进行了实验，包括PHOENIX14-T、CSL-Daily、大规模KSL以及Disaster and Safety KSL数据集。结果表明SMART在识别与定位两个任务上均有效，验证了MLLM引导的语义增强、多尺度时序建模以及CSLR-定位联合优化策略的优越性。实验验证了大规模基线数据训练下的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SMART的主要贡献在于：(1) 将MLLM生成的运动描述引入CSLR，缓解了弱序列标注下的语义与时序监督不足问题；(2) 提出多尺度时序适配器提升transformer的时序建模能力；(3) 通过CSFormer统一识别与定位，实现两类任务的互补增强。潜在局限包括对MLLM生成质量的依赖、跨语言/跨领域的鲁棒性以及对大规模数据与计算资源的实际需求有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Continuous sign language recognition (CSLR) aims to recognize gloss sequences from unsegmented sign videos under weak sequence-level supervision. However, existing methods rely on sentence-level gloss annotations, providing limited temporal and semantic guidance for fine-grained representation learning. Conventional video-text alignment also requires large batch sizes, making it inefficient for memory-intensive sign language video training. In this work, we propose SMART, an MLLM-guided temporal alignment framework for joint sign recognition and spotting. SMART uses MLLMgenerated motion descriptions as auxiliary semantic cues and performs stable videotext alignment under small-batch training. To improve temporal representation learning, we introduce a Multi-Scale Temporal Adapter that models temporal interactions during transformer encoding. For dense temporal localization, SMART incorporates CSFormer, a CSLR-guided spotting module that injects recognition-derived gloss evidence into a boundary-aware spotting network. This unified framework enables CSLR features to benefit spotting, while spotting supervision complements weak CTC-based recognition. Experiments on four sign language benchmarks, including PHOENIX14-T, CSL-Daily, Large-scale KSL, and Disaster and Safety KSL datasets, demonstrate the effectiveness of SMART across both recognition and spotting tasks.

</details>


### [38] [Pose-Anchored Optical Flow for Low-Latency Human Action Anticipation in Human-Robot Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25495)
*Lewis de Zoete Grundy,Chris McCarthy,Christopher Fluke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PoseOFF，一种以人体姿态为锚定的光流表示方法，通过在人体关节点周围提取局部运动信息，构建与人体运动学对齐的结构化运动表示，用于早期人类动作理解与动作预期。在多个基准数据集和骨干网络上验证了其在早期观测比例下的一致提升，且无需全幅运动处理，适用于实时和资源受限场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机交互（HRI）中，机器人需要在动作执行早期就解读人类意图，以做出安全、高效和自然的响应。然而现有动作识别方法存在两难：稀疏骨架表示缺乏细粒度的运动线索，无法捕捉丰富的手部、躯干等局部运动细节；而稠密光流虽然富含运动信息，但计算开销大，难以满足低延迟感知管线需求。因此需要一种既能保留细粒度运动线索、又足够高效的运动表示，以支持早期动作意图理解。

Method: PoseOFF 的核心思想是将运动特征提取"锚定"在人体姿态上。具体而言，它围绕每个关节点（joint）捕获局部运动信息，从而在语义上有意义的身体位置编码局部运动动力学，形成与人体运动学（kinematics）显式对齐的结构化运动表示。这种表示将稠密光流计算约束在姿态相关的局部区域，而非整幅图像，既保留了细粒度运动线索（弥补骨架表示不足），又规避了全幅光流的计算开销。该方法不依赖特定骨干网络，可灵活集成到不同动作预期（anticipation）架构中。

Result: 作者在多个基准数据集和多种骨干网络架构上评估了 PoseOFF 在动作预期（action anticipation）任务上的性能。结果显示其在识别精度上取得一致性改进，尤其在早期观测比例（early observation ratios）下提升明显。PoseOFF 使模型在观测更少动作序列的情况下达到可比较或更优的性能，凸显了其在早期预测上的有效性。同时，由于无需全幅运动处理，该方法在实时和资源受限场景下表现实用。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是提出 PoseOFF 这一以姿态锚定的光流运动表示，在骨架的稀疏性与稠密光流的计算成本之间取得平衡，为 HRI 中的早期动作意图理解提供了实用的中间表示。其意义在于证明了姿态中心的运动表示能增强交互机器人系统更早地推断人类动作，从而支持更响应性和预见性的机器人行为。可能的局限性包括：依赖人体姿态估计质量，早期阶段关节检测误差可能影响运动表示精度；局部光流区域的大小等超参数选择可能需针对不同场景调优。

Abstract: Human-robot interaction (HRI) requires robots to interpret human actions early in their execution in order to respond safely, efficiently, and naturally. However, many existing approaches to human action recognition rely either on sparse skeletal representations, which lack fine-grained motion cues, or dense optical flow, which can be computationally expensive for low-latency perception pipelines. In this paper, we propose PoseOFF, a pose-anchored optical flow representation that captures local motion information around human joints to support earlier human intent understanding. By conditioning motion feature extraction on human pose, PoseOFF encodes localised motion dynamics at semantically meaningful body locations, forming a structured motion representation that is explicitly aligned with human kinematics. We evaluate PoseOFF across multiple benchmark datasets and backbone architectures for action anticipation, demonstrating consistent improvements in recognition accuracy, particularly at early observation ratios. Our results show that PoseOFF enables models to achieve comparable or improved performance while observing less of the action sequence, highlighting its effectiveness for early prediction. Importantly, these gains are achieved without requiring full-frame motion processing, making the approach practical for real-time and resource-constrained settings. These findings suggest that pose-centred motion representations such as PoseOFF can enhance the ability of interactive robot systems to infer human actions earlier, supporting more responsive and anticipatory behaviour in human-robot interaction scenarios.

</details>


### [39] [OpenVeinNet: Robust Open-Set Finger Vein Verification with Dynamic Snake Convolution and Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25515)
*Sushrut Patwardhan,Raghavendra Ramachandra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出OpenVeinNet，一种面向跨数据集和开放集环境的手指静脉验证框架。方法结合动态蛇形卷积（提取局部曲线/管状静脉结构）与图卷积骨干网络（建模静脉区域间的长距离拓扑关系），并引入Centroid Angular Hybrid Loss损失函数（同时促进类内紧凑和类间角度分离），在五个公共手指静脉数据集上进行了一次留一数据集训练（leave-one-dataset-out）评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手指静脉验证是一种有前景的生物特征模态，因为血管模式位于体内、难以外部观察且对呈现攻击相对鲁棒。然而在开放集设置下（测试身份在训练中不可见，且未注册的探针必须在推理时被拒绝），可靠验证仍具挑战。现有方法大多在封闭集或同数据集内评估，缺乏跨数据集泛化能力，且对手指静脉的曲线形态与全局拓扑关系建模不足。论文旨在解决开放集、跨数据集场景下的手指静脉验证问题，该问题对真实部署中的安全认证至关重要。

Method: OpenVeinNet由三个核心组件构成：(1) 动态蛇形卷积（Dynamic Snake Convolution），通过自适应采样提取局部曲线状与管状静脉结构，能更好地贴合静脉的弯曲几何形态；(2) 图卷积骨干网络，用于建模静脉区域之间的长距离拓扑关系，捕获全局血管结构；(3) Centroid Angular Hybrid Loss，联合优化嵌入空间，同时促进类内紧凑性与类间角度分离，适用于基于余弦相似度的验证。该框架专门针对跨数据集与开放集指纹静脉验证设计。

Result: 实验在五个公共手指静脉数据集上进行：FV-300、MMCBNU、FV-USM、PolyU和VERA。采用leave-one-dataset-out训练策略，并在两种协议下评估：基于注册的未知拒绝（enrolment-based unknown rejection）和全主体验证。与手工特征及近期深度学习方法基线对比。结果显示OpenVeinNet在跨数据集泛化上表现强，取得一致的低等错误率（EER），并在固定误接受率（FAR）工作点下取得有竞争力的真接受率（TAR）。消融实验验证了自适应管状特征提取、基于图的关联建模及所提损失函数各自的贡献及其组合效果。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于将显式的局部静脉几何建模、全局血管拓扑关系建模与角度紧凑嵌入相结合，有效解决了开放集手指静脉验证问题。实验证明这种组合策略对跨数据集泛化有效。可能存在的局限性未在摘要中明确说明，但可推测包括：图建模的额外计算开销、对复杂采集条件（如光照、姿态变化）的鲁棒性，以及损失函数在极大规模身份库上的扩展性问题。

Abstract: Finger vein verification is a promising biometric modality for secure authentication because vascular patterns are internal, difficult to observe externally, and relatively resistant to presentation attacks. However, reliable verification remains challenging in open-set settings, where test identities are unseen during training and non-enrolled probes must be rejected at inference. This paper presents OpenVeinNet, a finger vein verification framework designed for cross-dataset and open-set evaluation. The proposed model combines Dynamic Snake Convolution with graph-based feature modelling. Dynamic Snake Convolution extracts local curvilinear and tubular vein structures using adaptive sampling, while the graph convolutional backbone models long-range topological relationships between vein regions. To improve the discriminative quality of the embedding space, we introduce a Centroid Angular Hybrid Loss, which jointly encourages intra-class compactness and inter-class angular separation for cosinesimilaritybased verification. Experiments are conducted on five public finger vein datasets: FV-300, MMCBNU, FV-USM, PolyU, and VERA. The method is evaluated using leaveonedatasetout training under both enrolmentbased unknownrejection and fullsubject verification protocols, and is compared with handcrafted and recent deep learning-based baselines. The results show that OpenVeinNet achieves strong cross-dataset generalisation, consistently low equal error rates, and competitive true accept rates at fixed false accept rate operating points. Ablation studies further confirm the individual and combined contributions of adaptive tubular feature extraction, graph-based relational modelling, and the proposed loss function. These findings indicate that explicitly modelling local vein geometry, global vascular relationships, and angularly compact embeddings is effective for openset finger vein verification.

</details>


### [40] [Asymmetric Cross-Modal Fine-Grained Visual Categorization: ACF-Net and the BirdPro Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25520)
*Bohan Deng,Shuo Ye,Zitong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ACF-Net，一种光流引导的框架，用于解决不对称跨模态场景下的细粒度视觉分类（FGVC）问题。该框架包含两个核心模块：光流引导运动模块（OFGM）用于捕捉运动敏感视觉线索并抑制无关背景干扰，以及非对称跨模态自适应融合模块（ACAF）用于估计弱匹配音视频对下的模态可靠性并进行不确定性感知的自适应融合。作者还构建了新的鸟类别音视频基准数据集BirdPro（含1,919段音频和11,965段视频，覆盖194种鸟类），实验表明ACF-Net在融合和错配设置下分别超越最强基线2.97%和1.92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的音视频跨模态细粒度视觉分类（FGVC）研究通常假设视频与音频严格同步且对应同一实例，但在真实场景（如无人机观鸟、野外生态监测）中，配对视频与音频往往不同步，甚至可能来自不同的个体或时刻，形成"非对称跨模态"场景。这种弱且模糊的跨模态对应关系给表示学习和模态对齐带来显著挑战，而这一问题尚未得到充分研究。作者指出，现有数据集大多缺乏大规模、非严格时间与实例对应条件下类别级音视频关联，这制约了该方向的研究进展。

Method: ACF-Net由两个关键模块构成。（1）光流引导运动模块（OFGM）：利用光流信息捕捉运动敏感的视觉线索，并通过抑制无关背景干扰增强视频中的判别性动态表示，从而提升视觉特征的运动区分能力。（2）非对称跨模态自适应融合模块（ACAF）：在弱匹配的音频-视频对条件下估计各模态的可靠性，并执行不确定性感知的自适应融合，提高类别级别的识别鲁棒性。该设计不依赖严格的跨模态对齐假设，而是通过模态可靠性估计与不确定性加权来应对弱对应关系。此外，作者构建了BirdPro基准数据集，包含1,919段音频和11,965段视频，覆盖194种鸟类，为不对称跨模态FGVC研究提供了大规模类别级音视频关联数据。

Result: 作者在构建的BirdPro基准数据集上对ACF-Net进行了广泛实验，与多个代表性基线方法进行比较。结果表明ACF-Net在所有对比方法中取得最佳性能，在融合设置（fused setting）下比最强基线提升2.97%，在错配设置（mismatched setting）下提升1.92%。这验证了光流引导的运动表征与不确定性感知的自适应融合在非对称跨模态FGVC中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献包括：（1）首次系统关注非对称跨模态FGVC问题，其中音视频对既不严格同步也可能不对应同一实例；（2）提出ACF-Net框架，通过光流引导运动模块增强判别性动态表示，并通过非对称跨模态自适应融合模块在弱对应条件下进行可靠性感知的鲁棒融合；（3）构建了大规模鸟类音视频基准BirdPro。局限方面，论文当前仅基于单一鸟类别数据集验证，方法的泛化性在其他物种或更广泛场景（如UAV实景感知）中仍需进一步验证；另外光流计算可能带来额外推理开销，实时性方面（如边缘部署）有待探讨。

Abstract: Audio-visual cross-modal Fine-Grained Visual Categorization (FGVC) aims to identify fine-grained categories by jointly leveraging visual and auditory information. However, FGVC under asymmetric cross-modal scenarios has received limited attention, where paired video and audio are not strictly synchronized and may not even correspond to the same individual or moment. Such weak and ambiguous cross-modal correspondence poses substantial challenges to effective representation learning and modality alignment. To address these issues, we propose ACF-Net, a novel optical flow-guided framework for asymmetric audio-visual fine-grained learning. ACF-Net consists of two key modules: Optical Flow-Guided Motion (OFGM) and Asymmetric CrossModal Adaptive Fusion (ACAF). OFGM captures motion-sensitive visual cues and suppresses irrelevant background interference, thereby enhancing discriminative dynamic representations in videos. ACAF estimates modality reliability under weakly matched audio-video pairs and performs uncertainty-aware adaptive fusion to improve category-level recognition robustness. To support research on asymmetric cross-modal FGVC, we further construct BirdPro, a new bird-oriented audio-visual benchmark, since existing datasets often lack large-scale category-level audio-video associations under non-strict temporal and instance correspondence. BirdPro contains 1,919 audio recordings and 11,965 videos covering 194 bird species. Extensive experiments show that ACF-Net achieves the best results compared with representative baseline methods, outperforming the strongest baselines by 2.97% and 1.92% in the fused and mismatched settings, respectively.

</details>


### [41] [Video-IFBench: Evaluating Instruction Following of Multimodal LLMs in Video Understanding Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25529)
*Hongbo Liu,Peixian Chen,Sihan Liu,Peiyuan Zhang,Kai Zou,Dian Zheng,Xiaoxing Hu,Yuhao Dong,Mengdan Zhang,Yunhang Shen,Haoyu Cao,Wei Liu,Weibo Gu,Xing Sun,Shengjie Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Video-IFBench，一个专门评估视频理解中多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）指令遵循能力（instruction following）的基准。该基准基于四类指令模板（单任务、多任务、选择、嵌套）构建了覆盖 32 种任务类型和 39 种约束类别的指令分类体系，采用半自动数据构建流程生成 1.5K 样本，并对 20 余个近期 MLLM 进行了大规模评估，发现视频指令遵循（尤其是多约束、语义约束和复杂条件分支结构）仍然是当前模型的显著短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解基准大多聚焦于任务准确率（如问答正确性），而忽略了模型是否真正遵循用户指定的多样化约束——包括基于视觉和音频内容的约束。真实世界视频理解场景要求模型既能正确理解内容，又能同时满足多种用户约束。作者指出这一"指令遵循"能力在视频理解领域研究不足、缺乏系统性评估，因此需要构建专门基准来填补该空白，为后续研究提供评测标准。

Method: 作者构建了一个包含四类指令模板的指令分类体系：single-task（单任务）、multi-task（多任务）、selection（选择）和 nested（嵌套）指令，覆盖 32 种任务类型和 39 种手工设计的约束类别（涵盖语义和格式两类约束）。为了降低标注成本，设计了半自动数据构建流水线，结合 MLLM 生成、程序化处理和人工验证三个环节，最终得到 1.5K 条高质量评估样本。该基准的独特之处在于强调约束满足（constraint satisfaction）而非单纯的内容正确性，且约束既包括视觉内容相关的，也包括音频内容相关的，可系统衡量模型在复杂条件分支（需根据视频内容选择正确路径）下的指令遵循能力。

Result: 作者对 20 多个近期 MLLM 进行了大规模评估。实验结果表明视频场景下的指令遵循对当前模型而言仍然具有挑战性，尤其在以下情况下显著退化：(1) 包含较多约束的指令；(2) 涉及语义层面的约束（相较于格式约束）；(3) 需要根据视频内容选择正确分支或路径的复杂条件结构。这些发现表明现有 MLLM 虽在视频内容理解上表现良好，但在精细约束遵循方面能力不足。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是首次提出面向视频理解方向的指令遵循基准 Video-IFBench，包括系统的指令分类体系、半自动高质量数据构建流水线和 20+ 模型的评测结果。该工作为视频理解中指令遵循能力的研究提供了标准化评测平台，并揭示了当前模型在多约束、语义约束和复杂条件结构下的显著局限。局限在于样本规模相对较小（1.5K），且指令约束体系虽覆盖面广但其在实际应用中是否具有生态效度（ecological validity）仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown strong performance in video understanding. However, their ability to follow instructions in this domain remains under-explored. Real-world video understanding requires models not only to interpret video content correctly, but also to satisfy diverse user-specified constraints. Existing benchmarks focus primarily on task accuracy rather than instruction adherence, leaving this capability insufficiently evaluated. To address this gap, we introduce Video-IFBench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating instruction following in video understanding, where models must satisfy diverse user-specified constraints, including those grounded in visual and audio content. We develop an instruction taxonomy with four templates, including single-task, multi-task, selection, and nested instructions, covering 32 task types and 39 manually designed constraint categories spanning both semantic and format requirements. To reduce annotation cost, we build a semi-automatic data construction pipeline that combines MLLMs, programmatic processing, and human verification, resulting in 1.5K samples. We conduct a large-scale evaluation of more than 20 recent MLLMs and show that video instruction following remains challenging for current models, especially for instructions with many constraints, semantic constraints, or complex conditional structures that require selecting the correct branch or path based on video content. We hope our work will facilitate future research on instruction following in video understanding scenarios.

</details>


### [42] [CropCop: An Auditable 120-Class Plant-Health Model from Benchmark Reconstruction to a Quantised Runtime Artifact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25539)
*Rana Muhammad Ahmed,Sabahat Abbas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CropCop是一个面向120类植物健康状态的闭集识别系统，从数据审计、泄漏控制、模型训练到量化部署的全链路均被验证。系统从117,546张审计图像出发，剔除跨分割边界的数据泄漏后冻结109,107张基准集（类别最大/最小比151.7:1）。DINOv3 ConvNeXt-Tiny参考模型达到98.51%准确率和96.87%宏F1；MobileNetV4 Conv-Medium经量化后生成22.60 MiB的ExecuTorch/XNNPACK PTE，直接执行时保持98.46%准确率和96.23%宏F1。论文核心贡献在于建立从数据集重构到最终量化产物直接执行的完整证据链，证明量化图与PTE仅有6/16,363个top-1决策改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文指出植物健康评分可能看似精确，却可能建立在重复图像家族、长尾标签空间或从未被评估的运行时文件之上——这是植物健康识别领域常见但被忽视的可信度陷阱。此类问题会导致：(1) 跨分割边界的图像泄漏使验证结果虚高；(2) 标签分布极端不平衡使宏指标失真；(3) 仅验证转换图而非最终部署的原生PTE文件，使模型精度承诺与实际运行产物脱节。这三点分别对应数据泄漏、指标可信度与部署保真度三个层面的漏洞，说明仅报告准确率并不足以证明系统的真实价值，需要建立完整审计链路。

Method: 论文方法分为四层证据链：(1) 语料重建与泄漏审计：对117,546张图像进行audit，确认3,233对跨分割边界的重复关系，据此拒绝继承的分割，冻结109,107张无越界泄漏的基准集（类别比值151.7:1）；(2) 参考模型：全量微调DINOv3 ConvNeXt-Tiny，作为强基线而非新方法证据；(3) 轻量模型：MobileNetV4 Conv-Medium衍生模型，明确不将精度作为新蒸馏方法的证据；(4) 部署保真度验证：采用validation-only的后训练量化，选择动态激活+per-channel权重量化，生成22.60 MiB的ExecuTorch/XNNPACK PTE，并直接执行该PTE而非转换图来评估。通过对比INT8转换图与PTE间的top-1决策差异（仅6/16,363变化）和配对分析的类平衡损失，验证软件运行时保真度。

Result: 在锁定的109,107图像内部测试集上：DINOv3 ConvNeXt-Tiny达到98.51%准确率和96.87%宏F1；MobileNetV4 Conv-Medium达到98.46%准确率和96.27%宏F1；量化后的22.60 MiB ExecuTorch/XNNPACK PTE直接执行时保持98.46%准确率和96.23%宏F1。INT8转换图与PTE之间仅6/16,363个top-1决策改变，配对分析显示类平衡损失温和。然而，探索性的post hoc水果标签切片分析定位到比聚合准确率所显示的更大的召回率下降，说明特定类别在量化后受影响更大。且结果显示系统仅验证了泄漏受控的内部识别与软件运行时保真度，未建立对未见农场、不同相机管线或物理Android硬件上的性能。

Conclusion: CropCop的主要贡献在于将"植物健康识别"的可信度问题转化为可验证的工程管线：通过严格的跨分割泄漏审计、类平衡冻结基准、以及直接执行最终量化PTE产品来建立端到端证据链。其量化保真度令人满意（INT8→PTE仅6个决策变化），且对长尾数据保持高宏F1。局限在于：(1) 实验局限于内部测试集，缺乏对未见农场、不同采集设备（相机管线）及真实Android硬件的泛化验证；(2) post hoc类别切片显示部分类别（如水果标签）在量化后召回下降被聚合指标掩盖，提示需要对细粒度类别做量化敏感性分析；(3) 未声称提出新的蒸馏方法，工程贡献大于算法创新。

Abstract: A plant-health score can appear precise while resting on duplicated image families, a long-tailed label space, or a runtime file that was never evaluated. We present CropCop, a closed-set recognition system spanning 120 operational plant-health classes and an evidence chain from corpus reconstruction to direct execution of the final quantised artifact. Starting from 117,546 audited images, we rejected the inherited partition after confirming 3,233 duplicate relationships across split boundaries and froze a 109,107-image benchmark with zero crossings among the audited trusted leakage groups and a 151.7 largest-to-smallest class ratio. A fully fine-tuned DINOv3 ConvNeXt-Tiny reference achieved 98.51% accuracy and 96.87% macro-F1 on the locked internal test. A compact MobileNetV4 Conv-Medium derivative achieved 98.46% accuracy and 96.27% macro-F1 without being presented as evidence for a new distillation method. Validation-only post-training quantisation selected dynamic activations with per-channel weights, and the final 22.60 MiB ExecuTorch/XNNPACK PTE achieved 98.46% accuracy and 96.23% macro-F1 when executed directly. Only six of 16,363 top-1 decisions changed between the converted INT8 graph and the PTE, while paired analysis showed a modest class-balanced loss; an exploratory post hoc fruit-label slice localized a larger recall decline than aggregate accuracy revealed. CropCop establishes strong leakage-controlled internal recognition and software-runtime fidelity; it does not establish performance on unseen farms, camera pipelines, or physical Android hardware.

</details>


### [43] [AdaVDR: Adaptive Tool Use and Reflection for Video Deep Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25559)
*Xintong Zhang,Xiaomeng Fan,Shilin Yan,Ekko He,Zicheng Liu,Zijian Zou,Guannan Zhang,Yuwei Wu,Zhi Gao,Hongwei Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出AdaVDR，一个自适应视频深度研究代理，通过自适应工具调用与反思机制，根据任务和视频类型动态选择工具使用策略，并仅在中间结果不可靠时才回溯纠正。作者构建了一个视频深度研究数据构建流程（事件/实体发现、外部检索、QA构建、工具轨迹组织），并引入模型感知的工具必要性过滤，为目标模型裁剪掉可跳过的工具链，生成适配模型能力的训练轨迹。基于SFT+RL（冗余感知奖励）训练后，在VDR-EE基准上优于所有评估的开源模型，并在VideoDR上与基础模型相比大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频深度研究任务需要同时理解视频内容并从开放Web检索外部知识来回答复杂问题。然而，不同的问题和视频需要不同的工具使用策略，不恰当的工具调用会产生错误结果；不确定的grounding和检索导致不必要的交互，增加延迟和推理错误。核心问题在于：现有方法无法自适应地决定何时调用工具、调用哪些工具，以及何时信任中间结果。这对事件相机/视频感知等实时系统尤为重要，因为工具调用的冗余和不可靠检索会显著影响推理效率与准确性。

Method: AdaVDR包含两大核心创新：(1) 自适应工具选择与反思机制——根据任务和代理自身能力选择工具，仅在不可靠中间结果需要纠正时回溯；(2) 视频深度研究数据构建管线。管线先在不同视频中发现检索相关的事件与实体，通过grounding和外部检索获取详细信息构建高质量QA对；再针对每个QA用任务特定提示把信息获取组织为工具使用轨迹，使不同问题/视频类型遵循不同grounding与检索策略；进一步引入模型条件工具必要性过滤，评估工具调用相对目标模型的视频理解与内部知识能否跳过，从而移除模型可绕过的工具或工具链，生成适配模型能力的轨迹。训练采用SFT加带冗余感奖励的强化学习，强化自适应工具调用与反思。

Result: 作者构建了VDR-EE基准，覆盖实体驱动和事件驱动两类问题，并构建训练数据。实验表明：AdaVDR在VDR-EE上表现优于所有被评估的开源模型；在VideoDR基准上相对其基础模型有大幅提升（substantially improves）。论文提供的是整体性结果而非逐任务细粒度指标。（具体数值未在摘要中给出。）

Conclusion: 主要贡献：(1) 提出自适应工具调用与反思的AdaVDR框架，解决视频深度研究中工具使用策略僵化、检索冗余和不可靠grounding的问题；(2) 提出完整的视频深度研究数据构建管线，包括模型条件工具必要性过滤，为不同模型定制训练轨迹；(3) 提出VDR-EE基准和冗余感知的RL训练方法。局限性：摘要未说明该方法在面对超长视频、多轮复杂推理或实时流式视频（如事件相机输入）时的时间复杂度和可扩展性，仍需验证。

Abstract: Video deep research answers complex questions by jointly understanding video content and retrieving external knowledge from the open Web. However, diverse questions and videos require different tool-use strategies, and inappropriate tool calls can produce incorrect results. Uncertain grounding and retrieval also make unnecessary interactions costly and error-prone, increasing latency and reasoning errors. To address these challenges, we propose AdaVDR, an adaptive video deep research agent with adaptive tool invocation and reflection. AdaVDR selects tools according to the task and its capabilities, and backtracks only when unreliable intermediate results require correction. To enable these capabilities, we develop a video deep research data construction pipeline. We first discover retrieval-relevant events and entities in diverse videos and acquire detailed information through grounding and external retrieval to construct high-quality QA pairs. For each QA, task-specific prompts organize the information acquisition process into a tool-use trajectory, allowing different question and video types to follow different grounding and retrieval strategies. We further introduce model-conditioned tool necessity filtering, which evaluates tool calls against the target model's video understanding and internal knowledge, removing tools or tool chains the model can bypass. This yields trajectories tailored to the target model's video understanding capability and knowledge. Using this pipeline, we construct training data and VDR-EE, a benchmark covering entity-centric and event-centric questions. We perform supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning with a redundancy-aware reward to strengthen adaptive tool invocation and reflection. Experiments show that our method performs best among the evaluated open-source models on VDR-EE and substantially improves over its base models on VideoDR.

</details>


### [44] [CrossMambaTuning: Synergistic Spatial and Cross-Layer Adaptation for Machine Vision Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25568)
*Haobo Xiong,Shaobo Liu,Kai Liu,Chongyang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CrossMambaTuning，一种基于状态空间模型（SSM/Mamba）与跨层交互机制的参数高效微调框架，用于将预训练的学习型图像压缩（LIC）模型适配到下游机器视觉任务。通过引入任务特定提示和多重跨层适配器，在冻结主干网络的同时实现跨层协调的细粒度特征融合，并以72%的参数压缩率达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型图像压缩（LIC）模型预训练后需要适配多个下游机器视觉任务，但传统方法直接在冻结主干中独立插入微调模块，缺乏显式的跨层协调机制，导致特征表达能力受限。当前方案存在两大痛点：(1) 部署成本高——每个下游任务都需要独立的完整模型；(2) 重训练开销大——独立微调模块忽视了不同层之间语义信息的关联性。如何在保持上游压缩能力的条件下，以参数高效方式实现跨层特征协同，是该研究的核心问题。

Method: CrossMambaTuning 包含两个核心组件：(1) 高效Mamba适配器（Efficient Mamba Adapter）：融合状态空间模型，配备任务特定提示（task-specific prompts）和多尺度分支（multi-scale branching），同时捕获局部特征与全局依赖关系，利用Mamba的长序列建模能力提升时空特征提取效率。(2) 尺度不变跨层适配器（Scale-Invariant Cross-Layer Adapter, SICA）：采用参数共享策略（parameter-sharing），在不同尺度间融合任务信息并降低参数冗余。整体在冻结的LIC主干上进行参数高效微调，通过SSM的线性复杂度特性保证推理效率。

Result: 在多个机器视觉任务上进行广泛实验，CrossMambaTuning 达到SOTA性能，相比现有最优方法将参数开销降低72%。论文公开了代码仓库。尽管摘要未列出具体数据集与指标细节，但结果表明该框架在压缩-视觉任务适配场景下，能以显著较低的可训练参数量取得最优下游任务精度。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：(1) 首次将状态空间模型与跨层交互机制结合用于LIC模型的下游适配微调；(2) 设计任务特定提示+多尺度分支的高效Mamba适配器，实现局部-全局特征协同；(3) 提出SICA跨层适配器以参数共享策略降低冗余。潜在局限：摘要未提供跨层协调机制的消融分析细节、Mamba在超长序列下的实际部署耗时对比，以及该方法在事件流/视频等高维时序数据上的泛化验证。

Abstract: To reduce deployment cost and retraining overhead, adapting pretrained learned image compression (LIC) models to downstream machine vision tasks has attracted growing attention. However, existing methods typically insert fine-tuning modules independently into frozen backbones, lacking explicit mechanisms for cross-layer coordination. To address this limitation, we propose a novel framework named CrossMambaTuning, which integrates State Space Models with cross-layer interaction mechanisms for parameter-efficient fine-tuning. Specifically, we design an efficient Mamba adapter equipped with task-specific prompts and multi-scale branching to precisely capture both local features and global dependencies. Furthermore, we introduce a Scale-Invariant Cross-Layer Adapter (SICA) utilizing a parameter-sharing strategy to fuse task information across different scales and reduce redundancy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CrossMambaTuning achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on multiple machine vision tasks, reducing parameter overhead by 72\% compared to SOTA methods. Code is available at https://github.com/rsr1123/CrossMambaTuning.

</details>


### [45] [MLLMCLIP: Feature-Level Distillation of MLLM for Robust Vision-Language Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25575)
*Jongsuk Kim,Qiyu Wu,Zhuoyuan Mao,Hiromi Wakaki,Junmo Kim,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MLLMCLIP，一种异构知识蒸馏框架，直接从生成式多模态大语言模型（MLLM）教师蒸馏多模态知识到判别式CLIP学生，完全绕开合成硬负样本pipeline，解决CLIP组合理解（属性-对象、关系结构）能力不足的问题。通过基于注意力的逐层token选择和基于CKA的蒸馏损失来桥接两类范式间的架构差异，在组合准确率上达到SOTA，同时保持零样本分类和图文检索性能稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等预训练视觉-语言模型虽然具备强大的零样本识别能力，但对组合性理解（特别是属性-对象绑定和关系结构）经常失效。现有缓解方案依赖用LLM+text-to-image级联生成合成硬负样本来增强训练，这带来了巨额pipeline开销（模型级联、数据生成、筛选），且合成数据质量受限。论文指出可以直接利用生成式MLLM中隐含的组合视觉-语言知识来增强判别式CLIP，避免中间合成数据，既降低成本又更高效的利用多模态知识。这一问题对组合认知、细粒度视觉理解等下游任务意义重大。

Method: 核心创新在于两个层面：(1) 使用异构蒸馏将MLLM教师（生成式架构）的知识迁移到CLIP学生（判别式架构），提出基于注意力的逐层token选择机制，用注意力权重挑选与学生token最相关的教师token，从而对齐两个异构模型的中间特征。(2) 引入基于CKA（Centered Kernel Alignment）的蒸馏损失，对选中的token特征进行相似性度量并优化，实现特征层级的对齐。整个方案完全不需要生成合成硬负样本，绕开昂贵的LLM+text-to-image级联pipeline，直接在特征层面注入MLLM蕴含的组合知识。

Result: 在组合性基准（如属性-对象组合、关系推理任务）上，MLLMCLIP相较现有CLIP增强方法取得state-of-the-art组合准确率。同时，在标准零样本分类和图文检索任务上也取得一致提升，说明特征级蒸馏不仅提升组合表示，还强化了通用视觉-语言表示能力。实验验证了attention-based token选择与CKA蒸馏损失在桥接异构架构上的有效性。

Conclusion: MLLMCLIP的主要贡献是提出了一条无需合成数据的CLIP组合能力增强路线，通过异构蒸馏直接利用MLLM教师的多模态知识，并设计了attention token选择+CKA损失两大技术来解决架构不匹配问题。它同时提升组合性与通用性，说明特征级对齐是增强VLM表示的有效途径。局限在于对教师模型选择、蒸馏层配置等超参数敏感，且依赖强大的生成式MLLM作为教师，其训练成本与可扩展性（大规模蒸馏）仍需进一步探索。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language models such as CLIP excel at zero-shot recognition but often fail at compositionality, particularly attribute-object and relational structures. Recent studies mitigate this issue by augmenting training with synthetic hard negatives generated by a cascade of large language models and text-to-image models, which incurs substantial pipeline overhead. We instead propose MLLMCLIP, a heterogeneous distillation framework that transfers multimodal knowledge directly from a generative Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) teacher into a discriminative CLIP student, bypassing synthetic data entirely. To bridge the architectural mismatch between the two paradigms, we introduce an attention-based per-layer token selection and a CKA-based distillation loss. Compared to prior CLIP-enhancement methods, MLLMCLIP achieves state-of-the-art compositional accuracy while delivering consistent gains on standard zero-shot classification and image-text retrieval, showing that feature-level distillation strengthens both compositional and general vision-language representation capability.

</details>


### [46] [VBVR-Pro: A Scalable and Verifiable Suite for Native Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26105)
*Junxiang Xu,Ruisi Wang,Fanyi Pu,Maijunxian Wang,Ran Ji,Tongxi Zhou,Chenyang Gu,Jing Zuo,Hongcan Xiao,Yimeng Geng,Wanqi Yin,Wei Chen,Oscar Qian,Zhengan Yan,Ziqi Huang,Haiwen Diao,Liang Pan,Bo Li,Xiangyu Fan,Dezhi Luo,Fengyuan Yu,Zehong Zhao,Qingying Gao,Tinghui Zhu,Yilan Zhang,Jingqi Tong,Pinyuan Feng,Zhengze Jiang,Letian Wang,Ziyu Guo,Renrui Zhang,Jieneng Chen,Sonia Joseph,Constantin Venhoff,Saman Motamed,Mengyue Yang,Chandra Sripada,Alan Yuille,Philip Torr,Lvmin Zhang,Vikash Kumar,Daniel Khashabi,Nikolaus Kriegeskorte,Raphaël Millière,Vincent C. Müller,Anyi Rao,Quan Wang,Ziwei Liu,Dahua Lin,Lei Yang,Hokin Deng,Zhongang Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VBVR-Pro，一个将"原生视觉推理"（以图像/视频生成本身作为推理媒介）引入可训练、可验证、可优化、可实验控制的闭环测试平台。其核心贡献包括：1) 300个程序化生成的视觉推理任务用于任务扩展，训练的模型在RISE-Video、MME-CoF-Pro、BabyVision等7个外部基准上展现强迁移能力；2) 基于确定性任务规则的验证奖励打分器，克服了主流VLM-as-a-judge范式的失败模式，并可作为大规模多任务强化学习的可靠奖励信号；3) 对30余种图像、视频和交错生成器的受控模态研究，揭示视频生成在持续时空状态追踪中表现最强，并探测到对视觉推理至关重要的"视觉原生轨迹"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生视觉推理将视觉生成视为推理本身的媒介——视觉状态（图像和视频）不仅是待理解的输入或待渲染的输出，而是超越语言的求解"基底"。然而这一方向受限于三大瓶颈：缺乏可扩展的训练任务、缺乏可靠的反馈信号、缺乏跨生成基底的受控比较。传统VLM-as-a-judge评估范式存在可复现性和可靠性的根本缺陷，而语言中心的多模态模型难以充分体现视觉生成在时-空推理中的独特价值。因此需要一套既能规模化训练、又能提供可信奖励、还能进行模态级受控研究的系统。

Method: VBVR-Pro采用三大技术支柱：(1) 任务扩展——将视觉推理转化为300个程序化生成的受控任务空间，覆盖多类型视觉推理场景，使训练模型具备跨套件迁移能力；(2) 可验证奖励——设计基于确定性、任务特定规则的奖励打分器（grounded scorers），替代依赖大型多模态语言模型（MLLM）评判的范式，作者系统研究了多种MLLM作为裁判时的反复故障模式，证明规则化打分器与人类判断的细粒度对齐更好，并可作为多任务强化学习（post-RL）的奖惩信源；(3) 机制研究——构建可控模态对比框架，对超过30种图像、视频和交错（interleaved）生成器进行消融与探测实验。核心发现包括：视频生成在需要持续时空状态追踪的任务中最强，交错生成提供了计算高效替代方案，且探测实验揭示了存在对视觉推理至关重要的"视觉原生轨迹"。

Result: 实验表明：在VBVR-Pro训练的模型在7个外部视觉推理基准（如RISE-Video、MME-CoF-Pro、BabyVision）上展现出超越任务套件本身的强泛化能力。在奖励机制方面，VBVR-Pro的规则化打分器相比VLM-as-a-judge方法在多项指标上与人类判断的细粒度对齐更优，并在大规模多任务强化学习后展现出更强的跨视觉推理任务性能。模态对比实验覆盖30+生成器，结果显示视频生成是持续时空状态追踪任务的最强基底，而交错生成在计算效率上更优。消融与探测实验提供了"视觉原生轨迹"存在性的证据，表明视觉生成内部存在可被学习的、独立于语言中介的推理通路。

Conclusion: VBVR-Pro的贡献在于第一次将原生视觉推理打造成一个可规模化、可验证、可强化学习优化、可模态级受控对比的完整研究闭环。其意义在于：既提供了300任务的扩展数据资产与高质量规则化奖励求值器，又通过系统性的模态机制研究明确了视频与交错生成在不同任务类型上的优势边界，并首次从探测和消融层面论证了视觉原生推理轨迹的存在。局限方面：虽然展示了跨7个基准的迁移，但300个程序化任务对真实世界复杂视觉推理的覆盖仍有限；奖励打分器依赖确定性规则设计，扩展至开放域任务需要额外的规则工程；对"视觉原生轨迹"的论证尚属于探测性证据，其神经层面的确切机制仍有待深化。

Abstract: Native visual reasoning treats visual generation as the medium of reasoning itself: visual states (i.e. images and videos) are not merely inputs to be understood or outputs to be rendered, but first-class substrates for problem solving beyond language. Yet progress remains bottlenecked by the lack of scalable training tasks, reliable feedback, and controlled comparisons across generative substrates. In this work, we introduce VBVR-Pro, a closed-loop testbed that makes native visual reasoning through generation trainable, verifiable, optimizable, and experimentally controllable. 1) Task scaling. VBVR-Pro turns visual reasoning into a controlled task space of 300 procedurally generated tasks. Models trained on VBVR-Pro show strong transfer beyond the proposed suite across seven external visual reasoning benchmarks such as RISE-Video, MME-CoF-Pro, and BabyVision. 2) Verifiable rewards. VBVR-Pro provides verifiable reward scorers for task-grounded evaluation. Through a systematic study of leading MLLMs as judges, we identify recurring failure modes of the prevalent VLM-as-a-judge paradigm. In contrast, the proposed scorers are grounded in deterministic, task-specific rules, achieve fine-grained alignment with human judgments. Importantly, they serve as reliable reward signals for large-scale multi-task reinforcement learning and demonstrate stronger post-RL performance across visual reasoning tasks. 3) Mechanism study. VBVR-Pro enables controlled modality studies across more than 30 image, video, and interleaved generators. Our analysis shows that video generation remains strongest for tasks requiring persistent spatiotemporal state tracking, while interleaved generation provides a compute-efficient alternative. Critically, ablations and probing suggest the presence of vision-native trajectories that are crucial to visual reasoning. We release all data, models, scorers, and code.

</details>


### [47] [V-Rubrics: Visual Faithfulness via Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25580)
*Shulin Tian,Minglun Li,Yuhao Dong,Hao Ding,Jiarui Yao,Haiwen Diao,Jingkang Yang,Hongyuan Zhu,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于视觉评分标准（Rubrics）的强化学习（Visual Rubrics-Based Reinforcement Learning, VRRL）方法，将参考回答分解为原子命题，并沿着三个维度——视觉忠实度（VF）、推理一致性（RC）和指令遵循度（IF）——对生成回答进行分项评分，从而为多模态后训练提供细粒度、结构化的部分信用（partial credit）信号，以解决视觉语言模型在视觉证据不足时仍生成流畅但未充分接地（grounded）回答的信用分配失败问题。作者基于Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct在OpenMMReasoner-SFT-874K上得到SFT检查点，构建了V-Rubrics 50K（50,248样本）训练集，并用基于评分标准的GRPO训练模型。实验表明，相比共享SFT基线和仅答案级GRPO，该方法在面向知识和视觉接地推理基准上取得更大提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文解决的问题是视觉语言模型（VLM）的"未充分接地幻觉"：模型能生成流畅的文本回答，但其内容并未得到视觉证据的充分支持——一个未支撑的物体、图表数值或中间推理步骤就可能使整体回答失效。作者将这一现象归因于多模态后训练中的信用分配（credit assignment）失败：标量结果奖励只能说明回答整体是否可接受，却无法指明哪些视觉事实是接地的、哪些推理步骤有效、哪些指令约束被遗漏。传统答案级奖励（answer-only reward）缺乏细粒度反馈，导致模型难以定位错误来源，因此需要一种能提供结构化、可局部化的奖励信号的监督范式。

Method: 技术上，本文的核心创新在于将参考回答分解为原子命题，并沿三个维度构建评分标准：（1）Visual Faithfulness（VF）评估视觉事实的接地性；（2）Reasoning Consistency（RC）评估推理步骤的有效性与一致性；（3）Instruction Following（IF）评估指令约束的满足程度。评分项提供结构化的部分信用，并在支持证据span可用时进行针对评分项的信用局部化（rubric credit localization）。训练流程：先在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上通过OpenMMReasoner-SFT-874K语料微调获得SFT检查点（沿用OpenMMReasoner的冷启动数据配方）；随后从17个视觉接地数据源构建V-Rubrics 50K数据集（50,248个样本），先应用规则过滤，再利用拒绝采样（rejection-sampling）分数推导样本难度，最后统一用Gemini-3-Pro在相同结构化提示与协议下对每个样本进行标注。训练时基于同一SFT检查点，采用分量式（component-wise）、前缀局部化（prefix-localized）的评分标准信用进行GRPO强化学习。

Result: 实验基于Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct模型，在OpenMMReasoner-SFT-874K上获得共享SFT基线，并比较三种设定：SFT基线、仅答案级GRPO、以及基于评分标准的GRPO。训练集为V-Rubrics 50K（50,248样本，来自17个视觉接地源，经规则过滤与拒绝采样难度排序，Gemini-3-Pro统一标注）。结果显示，基于评分标准的GRPO在共享SFT基线与仅答案GRPO之上均取得改进，最大增益出现在面向知识（knowledge-oriented）和视觉接地推理（visually grounded reasoning）基准上。作者据此认为评分标准（rubrics）是一种对视觉后训练有用的奖励抽象。

Conclusion: 本论文的核心贡献在于：将多模态后训练的奖励从粗粒度的标量结果奖励升级为细粒度、结构化的评分标准奖励（沿VF/RC/IF三个维度），并通过分量式、前缀局部化的信用分配解决视觉接地失败的信用分配问题；同时构建了V-Rubrics 50K训练数据集，为社区提供了可复现的大规模基于评分标准的视觉后训练数据。其意义在于证明了评分标准作为一种奖励抽象能有效缓解VLM的未接地幻觉。局限性方面，论文依赖Gemini-3-Pro进行标注（标注质量可能受基础模型限制），数据构建流程对SFT语料（OpenMMReasoner）和标注模型有较强依赖，且实验仅基于单一基础模型（Qwen3-VL-8B），泛化性尚需在更多模型规模与架构上验证。

Abstract: Vision-language models can produce fluent answers that are insufficiently grounded in the visual evidence: a single unsupported object, chart value, or intermediate inference can undermine an otherwise plausible response. We argue that this is a credit-assignment failure in multimodal post-training. Scalar outcome rewards indicate whether an answer is acceptable, but do not identify which visual facts are grounded, which reasoning steps are valid, or which instruction constraints are missed. We introduce Visual Rubrics-Based Reinforcement Learning, which decomposes reference responses into atomic propositions and scores generated answers along Visual Faithfulness (VF), Reasoning Consistency (RC), and Instruction Following (IF). The resulting rubric items provide structured partial credit and localize rubric credit when supporting evidence spans are available. We first obtain an SFT checkpoint by fine-tuning Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct on the public OpenMMReasoner-SFT-874K corpus, adapting OpenMMReasoner's cold-start data recipe. We construct V-Rubrics 50K, a 50,248-example training set from 17 visually grounded sources, by applying rule-based filters before deriving example difficulty from rejection-sampling scores and then annotating every example with Gemini-3-Pro under the same structured prompt and protocol. We train our model based on the same SFT checkpoint using component-wise, prefix-localized rubric credit. Experiments show that our rubricbased GRPO improves over both the shared SFT baseline and answer-only GRPO, with the largest gains on knowledge-oriented and visually grounded reasoning benchmarks. The results show rubrics as a useful reward abstraction for visual post-training.

</details>


### [48] [A Dual-Transformer for Multi-Camera View Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25601)
*Josep Cabacas-Maso,Carles Ventura,Ismael Benito-Altamirano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双Transformer架构与交叉注意力机制的多摄像机自动剪辑模型。该模型将任务解耦为两步：专用的时间编码器处理历史帧序列以构建丰富的记忆库，候选摄像机视角作为查询通过交叉注意力模块独立地检索该记忆库中的相关信息以完成自身评估。在TVMCE数据集上，该方法以56.60%的Precision@0.5大幅超越此前最优的37.16%，并在使用SwinV2轻量骨干网络时达到69.65%；此外，仅用目标视频20%的片段进行微调即可实现显著的编辑风格个性化适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多摄像机系统是现代媒体制作的基础设施，多摄像机编辑是一项关键任务——需要在每个时刻正确选择要播放的摄像机视角。现有的自动编辑方法在该任务上表现有限，尤其是缺乏对历史上下文的有效建模，难以在多个候选视角中做出合理决策。该问题的重要性在于：高质量的多摄像机编辑直接决定影视作品的叙事节奏和观看体验，而人工编辑成本高昂。本文旨在通过深度学习架构突破当前SOTA的性能瓶颈，并探索编辑风格个性化的可行性，为自动化影视后期制作提供技术支持。

Method: 本文提出一种Dual-Transformer架构，核心创新在于任务解耦：(1) 专用temporal encoder处理过去帧序列，构建对近期历史信息的丰富记忆表示；(2) 各候选摄像机视角作为query，通过cross-attention模块独立地对历史记忆进行查询，使每个候选视角能够自主挖掘与自身评估最相关的历史上下文。这种"编码器-记忆-查询"的设计允许不同视角同时且独立地进行打分评估，避免了视角间的信息干扰。研究人员还进行了backbone轻量化消融实验（SwinV2表现最佳），并探索了使用目标视频不同比例（如20%）的初始片段进行微调以实现编辑风格个性化的方案。

Result: 实验在TVMCE（TV Shows Multicamera Editing）数据集上进行，以Precision@0.5为主要评估指标。主要结果：(1) 所提Dual-Transformer模型达到56.60% Precision@0.5，相较此前最优结果37.16%有显著提升（约19.4个百分点的提升幅度）；(2) 轻量骨干消融实验中，SwinV2 backbone获得最佳性能69.65%；(3) 编辑风格个性化实验中，仅用目标视频20%的片段进行微调即可观察到Precision@0.5的可测量提升，表明该方法具备面向特定剧集或制作人的数据高效风格适配潜力。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于：(1) 提出了一种Dual-Transformer + Cross-Attention的架构，通过"历史记忆构建+候选视角独立查询"的解耦设计，在多摄像机编辑任务上实现了对既有SOTA的大幅超越；(2) 系统验证了轻量骨干网络在该任务上的可行性，SwinV2配置表现最佳；(3) 首次探索了多摄像机编辑模型的风格个性化适配，验证了少量数据微调即可改善风格匹配的数据高效方案。局限性在于：实验仅基于单一数据集（TVMCE），尚未验证跨数据集或跨题材的泛化性；个性化实验仅以单一指标评估，未涉及用户主观评分等更全面的风格一致性评估；个性化微调对"编辑风格"的量化标准也缺乏更精细的定义。

Abstract: Multi-camera systems are foundational to modern media production, and multi-camera editing is a critical task. This involves the proper selection of the appropriate camera view at each moment. In this paper, we propose a novel Dual-Transformer architecture with Cross-Attention that heavily outperformed the current SOTA models over the TVMCE dataset (TV Shows Multicamera Editing dataset). Our model decouples these tasks: (1) a dedicated temporal encoder first processes the sequence of past frames to build a rich memory of the recent history, and (2) the candidate camera views then act as queries to this memory via a cross-attention module, allowing each candidate to independently interrogate the historical context and find the most relevant information for its own evaluation.
  Our approach achieved 56.60% Precision@0.5, representing a substantial improvement over the prior best result of 37.16%. We further conducted an ablation study exploring the use of lightweight backbone architectures, where the SwinV2 backbone yielded the best performance, achieving 69.65% Precision@0.5.
  Using this best-performing configuration, we then investigated the feasibility of adapting the model to replicate the editing style of a specific human editor. To this end, we fine-tuned the model using varying proportions of the initial segment of a target video. Our results demonstrate that even with only 20% of the video used for fine-tuning, the model exhibited measurable improvements in Precision@0.5, indicating strong potential for data-efficient personalization of editing style adapted to each individual TV show or producer.

</details>


### [49] [When Should a Network Emit Geometry, and When Should It Detect It? Readout, Reconciliation, and Representation in Floorplan Vectorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25608)
*He Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统性地比较了同一floorplan重建网络（由栅格化平面图恢复墙体、开口与房间）的两种输出读取方式：自回归坐标序列解码 vs. 稠密热图检测+图组装。在真实扫描（CubiCasa5K）上检测方式在墙体各项指标全面胜出（F1各容差下+2.7/+5.1），且利用解码器从未使用的开口热图可不经重训将开口F1提升2.6倍；在干净矢量渲染上序列解码占优5-8点。此外提供两族输出的确定性融合（墙体F1提升7点）优于输入侧条件化的证据，并发布编辑代价评估指标、修正的CubiCasa5K标注及16,998平面图的CC BY 4.0基准ResPlan-FP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面图矢量化（从栅格图恢复墙体、开口与房间几何）存在两类主流输出表征：一类采用自回归坐标序列（序列模型流派），另一类在密集结/中心线热图上做检测并组装成图（检测流派）。以往文献常隐式假设输出表征的选择决定了系统性能优劣，但两种方案往往在不同训练数据、损失与后处理流程下比较，缺乏受控的公平对照。本文核心动机是在"同一训练网络、同数据同配方"这一受控条件下，科学地回答输出表征（sequence vs. detection）对墙体与开口重建质量的实际影响，同时探究两类输出在输入/输出层面的融合价值，并首次引入以"人工修正工作量"为导向的编辑代价指标来更真实地衡量线条/墙体质量。

Method: 方法核心是搭建一个共享主干网络，可同时产生两类输出：(1) 自回归坐标序列解码器（输出墙/开口/房间的几何坐标序列）；(2) 稠密junction热图、centerline热图与opening热图检测头+图组装后处理。作者在同一已训练网络上切换两种读取方式，排除架构/训练差异。同时设计多种融合与控制实验：(a) 确定性输出级融合（两热的组合）评估；(b) 输入级条件化（以一族模型输出作为另一族输入的三种形式，含两项ground-truth内容对照）检验"输入前馈增益"是否存在。另提出编辑代价指标（基于编辑距离/修正步数衡量人类修正草案所需工作量），并构建ResPlan-FP基准（16,998组带冻结划分的平面图、三条基线条目），同时发布经人工修正的CubiCasa5K标注。

Result: 实验以CubiCasa5K真实扫描与干净矢量渲染为数据。主要发现：(1) 真实扫描上检测方式在每个墙体指标上均优于序列解码（容差0.05处墙体F1高2.7，0.015处高5.1，配对bootstrap置信区间排除零）；(2) 读取解码器从未训练使用的opening热图可在不重训情况下将开口F1提升2.6倍；(3) 优势随平面图尺寸增大而增强，在小平面图上反转；在干净矢量渲染上，序列解码在其训练覆盖的渲染风格下领先5-8点，但完全域迁移时检测读取（经标定阈值）仍保持领先，且墨迹密度与平面大小均无法解释反转；(4) 在匹配数据与配方下，带reconciliation步骤的room-centric系统与wall-first序列模型达到相当墙体质量，说明输出表征的重要性低于常见假设；(5) 确定性两输出融合将墙体F1提升7点，而输入侧条件化在三种形式（含两个GT内容对照）下均无增益，说明先验在输出端有效、输入端无效。

Conclusion: 本文提供了首个在共享网络上受控比较检测式与序列式输出表征的平面图重建研究，结论对社区常见假设构成挑战：输出表征的绝对优劣并不存在，而取决于数据域、平面规模与训练覆盖；检测式在真实噪声扫描上更稳健，序列式在训练覆盖的干净渲染上更强。方法论贡献包括：编辑代价评估指标（以人工修正量衡量草案质量）、修正的CubiCasa5K标注、以及可复现的CC BY 4.0基准ResPlan-FP（含冻结划分与三条基线）。研究局限在于比较集中于墙体/开口几何，房间级端到端质量与下游应用场景（如施工/BIM）尚需进一步验证，且融合策略仅评估确定性融合，未覆盖端到端学得的融合方案。

Abstract: A network trained to recover the walls, openings, and rooms of a rasterized floorplan can produce its output in two ways: by emitting the geometry as an autoregressive coordinate sequence, or by detecting it on dense junction and centerline heatmaps and assembling a graph. We compare the two readouts on the same trained network. On real scans (CubiCasa5K) detection is better on every wall measure (+2.7 wall F1 at tolerance 0.05, +5.1 at 0.015; paired bootstrap intervals exclude zero), and reading an opening heatmap the decoder never used raises opening F1 by 2.6x without retraining. Within real scans the readout's advantage grows with plan size and reverses on small plans; on clean vector renders sequence decoding is better by 5 to 8 points where its training covered the render style, while under full domain shift the readout, given calibrated thresholds, stays ahead; neither ink density nor plan size explains the reversal. With matched data and recipe, a room-centric system with a reconciliation step and a wall-first sequence model reach comparable wall quality, so the output representation matters less than is usually assumed. A prior from the other family helps at the output but not at the input: deterministic fusion of the two outputs raises wall F1 by 7 points, whereas conditioning one model on the other's output gives no gain in three forms, including two ground-truth-content controls. We also provide an edit-cost metric that scores a draft by the human work needed to correct it, corrected CubiCasa5K annotations, and ResPlan-FP, a CC BY 4.0 benchmark of 16,998 plans with frozen splits and three baseline tracks. Code, the benchmark, and the corrected annotations are available at https://github.com/Cyprinus12138/fpvec-lab

</details>


### [50] [On the Separation of Human and AI-Generated Images in CLIP Embedding Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25609)
*Andrea Asperti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文报告并解读了CLIP（对比语言-图像预训练）表示中的一个此前未报道的现象：人类绘画与AI生成绘画在没有受监督的辨别目标的情况下，会自发地沿联合嵌入分布的若干主导主成分方向分离。作者的目标不是利用该现象进行检测，而是对其进行解释——系统性地结合可解释图像表示与梯度反演，将这种分离追溯到图像域。研究发现，全局图像属性、简单局部统计等直观解释均被逐步排除，而多尺度散射可部分而不足地解释该现象；直接反演则揭示大幅沿主导方向的位移可由对人类几乎不可感知的扰动诱导，表明CLIP所利用的视觉证据与人类感知之间存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往针对AI生成图像检测的研究多聚焦于如何利用判别信号构建检测器，而本文关注一个更根本的问题：CLIP嵌入中人类与AI绘画的自发分离究竟编码了何种视觉信息，以及这些信息能否通过可解释手段从特征空间回溯到图像域。这一问题的意义在于：其一，它揭示了无监督表示中可视证据与人类感知之间可能存在的系统性偏差；其二，阐明这种分离的成因对理解CLIP的视觉先验、判别空间结构和人类美学判断与人工视觉的关系具有基础性价值，相关问题在事件/仿生视觉和鲁棒感知中也具有重要意义。

Method: 作者采用"渐进式调查"方法作为实验探针，将分离现象作为因变量，系统地测试各类可解释图像表示能否解释特征空间中的关系。具体技术路径包括：(1) 对人类/AI绘画的联合嵌入分布执行PCA等主导方向分析，确认两个类别的自发分离；(2) 逐步引入越来越具表达力的统计描述子——从全局图像属性（如色彩、亮度和全局统计量）到简单局部统计，再到多尺度散射变换（multiscale scattering），以消融哪些图像信号足以导致该分离；(3) 采用基于梯度的直接反演（gradient-based inversion），把嵌入空间中的位移映射回图像扰动域，以观察生成这些位移所需的像素级变化及其对人眼的可感知性。多尺度散射作为最丰富、最具解释力的可解释表示被重点考察，但其只能提供部分解释，说明分离背后还涉及分布式的多尺度图像结构。

Result: 健壮性实验与不断增加的统计描述子表达能力逐级排除了基于全局图像属性和简单局部统计的解释，指出分离根源在于分布式多尺度图像结构。多尺度散射是所考察的最具信息量的可解释表示，但对完整现象仍只能给出部分解释。直接反演获得关键互补观察：沿CLIP主导方向的大幅嵌入位移可由对人类观察者几乎不可察觉的图像扰动诱导出来，证明隔离所需的方向对感知显著性极低的图像变化高度敏感。这些实验共同说明该现象无法由单一直观图像特征完全解释，而指向跨越尺度的结构信息，且其中一部分视觉证据对人类感知是隐性的。

Conclusion: 本工作的核心贡献在于：首次报道并系统解读了CLIP表示中人类与AI绘画的自发分离现象，确立了"不以检测为目标、而以解释为目标"的研究范式；通过渐进消融与反演实验，将解释空间从全局统计收窄到多尺度结构，并揭示了CLIP的视觉证据在感知显著性上与人眼不一致的事实。其局限性在于：散射表示只能部分解释现象，说明存在尚未被该表示捕获的额外结构信息；直接的、完备的机制性解释仍未给出。该工作对理解人工视觉与人类视知觉/美学判断的差异提出了更广泛的开放问题，对评估生成图像检测的可靠性以及人机视觉对齐研究具有启示意义。

Abstract: We identify a previously unreported phenomenon in CLIP representations: human and AI-generated paintings spontaneously separate along the dominant principal directions of their joint embedding distribution, without any supervised objective designed to distinguish the two classes. Rather than exploiting this phenomenon for detection, our objective is to interpret it: we seek to identify the visual information underlying the separation and to trace it back from the embedding space to the image domain. We pursue this objective through a progressive investigation combining interpretable image representations with gradient-based inversion, used systematically as an experimental probe of the relationships identified in feature space. Robustness experiments and increasingly expressive statistical descriptors progressively rule out several intuitive explanations based on global image properties and simple local statistics, and point instead to distributed multiscale image structure. Multiscale scattering provides the most informative interpretable representation considered, but offers only a partial account of the phenomenon.
  Direct inversion provides a complementary and striking observation: substantial displacements along the dominant CLIP directions can be induced by image perturbations that remain nearly imperceptible to human observers, showing that the directions involved in the separation are highly sensitive to image variations with very low perceptual salience for humans. Taken together, these results reveal a significant difference between the visual evidence reflected in CLIP representations and that readily accessible to human perception, raising broader questions about the relationship between artificial and human vision and, ultimately, between artificial and human aesthetic judgment.

</details>


### [51] [Plans You Can Check: Verifier-Grounded Learning of an Open-Weight Planner for Executable Video-Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25622)
*Haoyu Wang,Cheng Feng,Liuyang Bian,Ruiyang Huang,Lei Wei,Yafei Wen,Xiaoxin Chen,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RefineCut，将实用视频编辑视为可执行的视频编辑规划（executable video-editing planning）决策层问题。与依赖封装前沿模型的流程系统不同，RefineCut 训练了一个紧凑的开源 8B 规划器，通过结构化补丁操作类型化时间线（涵盖片段选择、裁剪、排序、转场、时长与音乐对齐），并由确定性验证器应用每个补丁并对照显式约束账本检查。由于编辑没有单一 ground-truth 修复，该方法不像直接模仿教师，而是回放多教师分支并通过验证器筛选验证器最佳修复作为监督；第二阶段的 RefineCut-Evo 让学生在闭环验证器与任务评分规则下对自己的修复打分，训练高置信度偏好对，使最终 8B 规划器在推理时完全无需教师调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 问题在于现代视频编辑不仅是像素生成，更关键的是决策层：编辑者需要将简短需求、素材池、音乐元数据和硬约束转化为可执行的时间线。现有方法多为封装提示式前沿模型的流程系统，依赖昂贵且不可解释的大模型，且编辑缺少单一 ground-truth 修复，直接模仿教师会带来监督噪声。研究这一决策层对于实现高效、可部署的实际视频编辑系统具有重要意义，需要训练一个紧凑开源规划器来执行这种结构化规划。

Method: RefineCut 的核心技术包括：(1) 将视频编辑规划形式化为结构化补丁序列，对类型化时间线执行片段选择、裁剪、排序、转场以及时长与音乐对齐；(2) 使用确定性验证器（deterministic verifier）应用每个补丁并对照显式约束账本（constraint ledger）检查；(3) 提出验证器回放蒸馏（verifier-replayed distillation）：回放每个多教师分支，仅保留验证器评分最高的修复作为监督，避免直接模仿不可靠教师；(4) 第二阶段 RefineCut-Evo让 8B 学生用验证器和任务评分规则对自己生成的修复打分，并训练高置信度偏好对，从而在推理时形成闭式验证器循环（closed verifier loop），无需任何教师调用。

Result: 在 RefineCut-Bench（3,578 个任务、7,971 条带说明的片段、499 首音乐曲目和显式约束账本）上，验证器回放蒸馏将规划器在协议特定的视频编辑分数（Video-Editing Score）上从 0.620 提升到 0.858；RefineCut-Evo 达到 0.924。增益可迁移至 Llama-3.1-8B 和 GLM-4-9B；在相同的闭式验证器循环中，8B 规划器达到或超过其前沿教师模型的性能。代码与 RefineCut-Bench 已公开。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：首次将视频编辑作为可执行规划问题处理并引入验证器回放蒸馏与自评分偏好训练，在无需教师推理的闭式循环下实现紧凑模型对齐或超越前沿教师，性能提升显著且具备跨模型迁移能力。局限在于方法依赖可形式化的约束账本和确定性验证器，对于难以显式编码的编辑审美或语义约束，评估与监督信号的质量可能受限，且 8B 模型规模虽紧凑但仍需进一步评估真实部署场景下的资源开销。
